Arm 2: with two or more chunks the ppN prime runs `prime_cache_hyper_pp2_pipelined` on two host threads over disjoint stage caches, bit-identical; it declines by name on `MEMRA_PRIME_PIPE=0` or a vision overlay. Prints `[prime-v2] arm1 engaged` and `[prime-v2] arm2 pipelined`. Gate `glm5-prime-v2-gate`, reds `MEMRA_PRIME_V2_GATE_RED=schedule` or `=pipe`. Rollback: unset. | [LANE.md](../research/b200-prefill-roofline-20260902/LANE.md), [GLM5-B200-DOORS.md](decisions/GLM5-B200-DOORS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_prime_v2), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_hyper_prime_v2) |
| `MEMRA_HYPER_SUFFIX_PRIME` | OFF | `1` lets a carried suffix (prefix restore, reuse hit, continuation) on a hyper-connections trunk (glm5_next) ride the prime program instead of tokenwise `decode_step`, lifting the `eager_mono && carried` veto at both prefill sites (`carried_suffix_primes`). Engagement logs `[suffix-prime] ENGAGED`; off, hyper trunks log `[suffix-prime] DECLINED` once per admitted request. Under the flag, a prompt token that still walks tokenwise logs `[suffix-prime] TOKENWISE (flag on)` and blocks both capture sites from publishing. gemma stays vetoed. It changes the suffix's numeric program; launch recipes arm it with `MEMRA_PREFIX_LATENT`. Rollback: unset. | [glm5-prefix-latent2-20260901](../research/glm5-prefix-latent2-20260901/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_hyper_suffix_prime) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_MAX_PROMPT` | unset (unlimited) | Route policy door, glm5 route only: `<tokens>` sends every glm5 spec-eligible request whose prompt is longer than the cap to the plain route. Composed after every correctness exclusion, so `[glm5-spec] route=plain ... reason=prompt-above-spec-max` appears only when the request would otherwise have routed spec; one announce line per process. `0`, empty or junk means unset. The dspark and qwen routes keep their own admission. Rollback seam: unset. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_max_prompt) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PENALTY` | unset (off) | `=1` admits penalized requests (any non-identity `repetition_penalty`, `frequency_penalty` or `presence_penalty`, greedy and sampled) to the glm5 DFlash2 spec route; off, they serve plain (`reason=penalized`). On: each verify round penalizes a device copy of the verify rows over the evolving window (`penalize_logits_rows_inc`), and greedy argmax, sampled p, bonus and residual read that copy; the draft stays unpenalized. The device pass is the host sampler's arithmetic bit for bit, so greedy keeps spec-vs-plain tape identity. Announce `[glm5-spec] penalty arm ENGAGED (MEMRA_SPEC_PENALTY=1)`. Read per session creation. Rollback: unset. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_penalty) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_WARM` | unset (off) | `=1` admits a glm5 prefix-hit carrier whose entry has no usable drafter tail (`reason=no-drafter-tail`): the drafter re-arms cold at the restored boundary (`DflashKv::new_cold_at`, context floor = the restored prefix) and fills from the suffix prime's tap rows on; the clipped round attention raises its window floor so empty rows are never scored. Verify arbitrates, so only acceptance can move, never output. Requires `MEMRA_GLM5_SPEC_PREFIX=1` (with `MEMRA_PREFIX_LATENT=1`); the legacy continuation pool keeps serving plain. A refusal prints `reason=warm-cold-drafter-refused`. Read per request. Rollback: unset. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_warm) |

### Step

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| **step37 serving defaults** (not a flag) | armed for the SlidingGatedMoe program (step37); off for every other family | The step37 numeric profile (`NumericProfile::step37_serving`, installed at model load) flips the unset default to on for `MEMRA_STEP_GEMM_PRIME`, `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER`, `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL`, `MEMRA_TCOL_FFN`, `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2`, `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8`, `MEMRA_W8_HYBRID`, `MEMRA_SWA_RING`, `MEMRA_MTP_HEAD_NVFP4`, `MEMRA_SPEC_HEAD_ROWS`, `MEMRA_Q8T_WONCE`. Co-loaded families read off at every door; the W8 hybrid half engages only in step37 launches. `MEMRA_SWA_RING` is armed process-wide, read per plan. Per door, `=1` forces on and `=0` is the rollback seam. `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS` is not in the set (it breaks server priming). Spec policy knobs such as `MEMRA_SPEC_K` stay serve config. | [step37-bankv2-removal-20260829](../research/step37-bankv2-removal-20260829/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_gemm_prime) |
| `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT` / `MEMRA_OPROJ_TAIL` / `MEMRA_SHEXP_SPLIT` | unset (off) | Step TP probes, each `1` to engage: `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT` splits the LM head matvec, `MEMRA_OPROJ_TAIL` defers the output-projection join into the residual/norm consumer, `MEMRA_SHEXP_SPLIT` splits the shared-expert down rows at t=1. Each falls back when its exact geometry is ineligible (`decode.rs`, `tp.rs`, `hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_NO_LOCAL_SHADOW` | unset (off) | `1` skips the Step TP eager local-KV shadow gathers and appends while still advancing their lengths. Spec and MTP scratch read those local contents, so this is a decode-only diagnostic door (`tp.rs`, `pp.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS` | off | `1` primes a fresh text-only prompt through the same-session t-row walk in chunks of `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS_T` rows (one rope/append, one fa, one o_proj join and one t-row MoE sweep per rank per layer per chunk), each row the t=1 decode program bit for bit; gate: token identity against `MEMRA_PRIME_TOKENWISE=1`. Suffix, vision and dflash-tap primes fall through. Requires `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER` and `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL`. Not family-armed: on the server route the walk leaves the sub-32 tail chunk out of the distributed KV and the request fails with `cache lengths diverged`, so the grouped GEMM prime (`MEMRA_STEP_GEMM_PRIME`) serves that route. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prime_trows) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS_T` | 8 | Chunk width for `MEMRA_PRIME_TROWS`, validated to 2..=8 by `parse_prime_trows_width`: an invalid value fails the request instead of clamping. Wider chunks are refused because the runtime-T tcol kernel holds at most 8 columns. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prime_trows_t) |
| `MEMRA_Q8T_WONCE` | on for step37 (family profile), off for other families | Routes the q8 t-column verify kernels to their weight-once `_tw` twins (`qkv_rp_tw`, `b4_rp_tw`, `rows_tw`, t <= 8): one row grid, each weight int4 loaded once and dotted against all t columns, instead of the `_t` forms whose column grid axis plus `__ldcs` (evict-first) loads re-read shared weights from DRAM once per column. Per-column float program unchanged, so output is bit-identical. `matvec_bf16_dual_silu_rows` (bf16 shexp) is not covered. Armed by the step37 serving defaults; `=1` forces on for any family, `=0` is the rollback seam. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q8t_wonce) |
| `MEMRA_ROPE_ROWS` | on (any value but `0`), engaged only on the `MEMRA_STEP_TP_BATCH` walk | Full t-row attention pass of the batched walk: rope/append, fa, combine and the o_proj join in 3 launches per rank per layer over the shared 6-word session table, with positions staged on the first engaged layer. `0` reverts to the per-row stash flow. Gate: TOKFP identity against the replay baseline at B=2, 4 and 8. `MEMRA_ROPE_ROWS_LAYER` limits it to a layer or range. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_rope_rows) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2` | step37 models: on; every other model: off | Tri-state step37 door (`1` forces, `0` kills). T-row verify attention: the verify walk stashes each column's post-rope q and gate rows, then one `fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw_rows` per rank attends every row, each (row, head, split) block running the t=1 dcw program with that row's own ring, length, base and split geometry, so rows are bit-identical at any t in 2..8, capped SWA included. The combine is the t=1 kernel over t*n_head stacked heads. The launcher embeds the big-rig split ladder and refuses env split overrides. Requires `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER` and `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL` (step37 defaults too). Gate: run-spec self-consistency at K=1..8. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_fa2) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_HEAD_ROWS` | on for step37 (family profile), off for other families | Batches the eager verify tail's LM head over its t columns instead of running it at m=1 once per column, so the head is streamed once per verify pass, not t times. Keeps the t=1 numeric class: a `FloatBf16` head at 1..=32 rows routes to `matvec_bf16_rows_into` (the decode head program per row, never cuBLASLt), the W8 q8 mirror is bit-identical to single-row calls, and `rms_norm` is row-wise. Armed by the step37 serving defaults; `=1` forces on for any family, `=0` is the rollback seam (the per-column loop). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_head_rows) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER` / `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL` / `MEMRA_TCOL_REFKERN` | `EAGER`, `TCOL`: on for the step37 SlidingGatedMoe program, off elsewhere; `REFKERN` off | Step serving-class verification. `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_EAGER` replays the live t=1 program row-outer; `MEMRA_SPEC_VERIFY_TCOL` adds T-column QKV amortization at T=2..8 and is subordinate to `EAGER`; `MEMRA_TCOL_REFKERN=1` runs the per-column t=1 reference kernel for T-column slabs, diagnosis only. `EAGER` and `TCOL` are tri-state: `1` forces on for any family, `0` is the kill switch, unset takes the model's step37 serving default (`spec.rs`, `tp.rs`). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_verify_eager) |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_BATCH` | on | On: the step35 batched trunk (`step35_decode_batch_layers`: per-layer head count, partial RoPE, per-session SWA views and head gate at m=B, batched over weights and per-session over KV) decodes B=1..8. `0` disables it and caps serving chunks at B=1; on PP-N that B=1 is refused too, because the eager B=1 trunk is a different numeric class and crossing it made greedy bytes depend on load history. An unsplit placement keeps its eager B=1 route. Also the `b2geo35c` canary seam. Rollback only; never off in a measured default run. | [step35-batch-20260808](../research/step35-batch-20260808), [p0iso-20260810](../research/p0iso-20260810), [b1fix-20260810](../research/b1fix-20260810), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step35_batch) |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_DRAFT_DCW` | on (any value but `0`); read once per process | Step-3.7-Flash MTP draft attention runs `mtp_step35_attn_dcw` (`append_kv_quantized_dcw` plus `fa_decode_dcw`), whose SWA view derives from device state (`len_d`, `base_d`, window 512), so the per-session draft-chain CUDA graph captures instead of warning and running eager. Eager and captured drafts share one launcher at bucket `min(cap, window)`, so graph-versus-eager parity holds by construction; verify arbitrates every byte. Needs the v3-vec class (`fa_v3_active`, default KV formats); ineligible shapes fall back to the off arm. `=0` and a restart is the rollback seam (host-len `mtp_step35_attn` plus the named capture refusal). Boot receipt: `[mtp-geom] arm=dcw`. | [RESULTS.md](../research/step37-draft-graph-serving-20260830/RESULTS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step35_draft_dcw) |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_PRIME_BATCH` | on | On: step35's cross-request fresh-prompt concat-prime range walk batches the owning-stage weight-streaming trunk at `m=sum(T)` while each request keeps its own attention geometry, absolute `seq_end`, KV append and cache; under PP-N each range runs on its owning stage. `0` restores the named refusal and serialized single primes. With `MEMRA_STEP_TP_PREFILL=1` the concat path refuses before cache mutation and the server runs the per-session grouped path. Carried continuation batches refuse and fall back (they need per-request queued-after metadata). Gate: `step35-prime-batch-gate.sh` (`pbatch35`, `pbatch35c`). | [primebatch-20260808](../research/primebatch-20260808/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step35_prime_batch) |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_PRIME_BATCH_TSEND` | off | Canary seam, not a performance knob: `=1` restores the pre-fix chunk-local `seq_end` (`ts[s]`) in the step35 batched prime instead of the request-absolute `cache.pos + t + queued_after`. Step35's SWA arm keys only on `seq_end > win` (win 512), so a chunk shorter than the window at a nonzero base would pick the unwindowed FA arm and attend outside the window. The fix is unconditional; the seam is chunk- and suffix-variant by construction, which gives the suffix byte-identity gate its teeth (as `MEMRA_STEP35_SWA_TKV` does for the walk). Never on in a measured default run. | [gemm-suffix-20260828](../research/gemm-suffix-20260828/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step35_prime_batch_tsend) |
| `MEMRA_STEP35_SWA_TKV` | off | `1` restores step35's pre-fix SWA prefill predicate (keyed on the chunk's own `t_kv > win` instead of the request's `seq_end > win`) with the raw, unaligned SWA view offset. Rollback seam of the step35 chunk-invariance fix and the `chunkinv35` gate's canary injection: chunk-variant by construction, so `MEMRA_PRIME_CHUNK` moves the logits under it. step35 only; never on in a measured default run. | [step35-chunkfix-20260807](../research/step35-chunkfix-20260807), [chunkinv-step35-GAP2-CONFIRMED-20260807.txt](../research/step37-p2-20260806/raw/chunkinv-step35-GAP2-CONFIRMED-20260807.txt), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step35_swa_tkv) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_GEMM_PRIME` | on for step37 (family profile), off for other families | Step37 prime chunks (prefill, t >= 16) route the routed MoE through one grouped f16 GEMM per projection over the resident NVFP4 banks (`run_tensor_parallel_routes_nvfp4_prime_grouped`) instead of per-token device routes: host CSR from the sigmoid router oracle, per-rank pointer tables, gate/up macros applied before silu, down partials joined in pinned order, fixed slot-order scatter (no atomics). Grouped-prefill numeric class, never byte identity. Needs `MEMRA_MOE_F16G=2`. On the server route it is the only prime that works: memra-server refuses `=0` at boot for step37-class models (`dead_prime_kill_switch_refusal`); `=0` stays usable offline (run-gen, engine bins). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_gemm_prime) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_GEMM_PRIME_SUFFIX` | on (any value but `0`), read per call | A continuation prime (`cache.pos > 0`: a rewound session suffix or a prompt remainder split across scheduler ticks) rides the batched GEMM prime like a fresh prompt (GEMM trunk at m = chunk plus grouped NVFP4 MoE). `=0` puts continuations back on the walk while fresh primes keep the fast path; `MEMRA_STEP_GEMM_PRIME=0` is the whole-path seam. Byte identity is not its gate (every prime path is m-dependent); admission is sampled quality, the cache-on twin and batched-prime acceptance. Engagement: `[gemm-prime] ENGAGED t=.. base=..` with `base>0` is the suffix arm, `[gemm-prime] WALK ...` the other; a log with neither is an instrument fault. | [step37-sampled-quality-20260828](../research/step37-sampled-quality-20260828/), [PREDICTION.md](../research/gemm-suffix-20260828/PREDICTION.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_gemm_prime_suffix) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_BATCH` | off | `1`: the t-row batched step TP walk for 2 to 8 rows. Per layer: one t-grid attn norm and one weight-amortized QKV over all rows, per-row attention on each row's own session cache (the t=1 program), o_proj deferred and joined once, one t-row routed-expert sweep with one combine per rank, and the exact t=1 shared expert per row; boot line `[step-tp-batch-trow]`. Per-session greedy is bit-equal to the layer-major-b1 replay. With `MEMRA_TCOL_TWINS` off the bf16 tcol arm uses the runtime-T kernel at every width; a step37 model runs that arm only with `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8=0`. Requires `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED=1`, native P2P, all layers step TP and the big-rig fa ladder; other shapes take the replay. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_batch) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DCW` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: device-counter-windowed step TP decode attention. KV append lands at `row = len_d - base_d` in-kernel, `inc_i32` advances the device counter in-stream, and `fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw` derives the attention view from the counters; at matching `(bucket, split_keys)` it is bit-identical to the host-planned path. Rebase tokens and sub-vec-floor contexts fall back to the host-row path. Requires the v2 driver and `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED=1`. Gate: 200-token identity against the dcw-off tape plus the 12-boot battery. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_dcw) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DECODE_V2` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: the v2 rank-local Step decode-attention driver, with the v1 kernels, operand values and canonical O reduction order on a persistent per-runtime workspace with evented cross-stream ordering, so each layer and token does one cuMemAlloc (the output row), no host round trip and no host stream sync. Requires `MEMRA_STEP_TP_F32_MIRROR=1` or `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED=1` (refused otherwise) and the replicated decode input; `MEMRA_PARALLEL_TP_ATTENTION=1` requires it. Prefill and non-attention programs are unchanged. Gate: v1-vs-v2 greedy token identity plus the run-gen argmax gate across a boot battery. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_decode_v2) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_DEV_ROUTER` | off | Strict `0`/`1` (other values error): route the step TP MoE selection on device (`moe_router_sigmoid_topk`) and feed the device-routed expert program, removing the per-layer host logits readback, the last per-layer host sync in the MoE path and a prerequisite for whole-token graph capture. Engages at t=1 with `MEMRA_STEP_NVFP4_DEV_ROUTES=1` on an NVFP4 bank. Numeric class: tie-breaking may differ from the host router; acceptance is the run-gen argmax gate plus the 12-boot battery. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_dev_router) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_KV_RESTORE` | off | `1` re-enables the canonical-to-rank byte copy in the spec verified-prefix restore on step TP layers; layers whose rank rows are written on device (`rows_external`) are still skipped. Off, the restore copies no bytes into rank rows and only rewinds the rank length on a partial accept: on the dcw/fa2 verify path the canonical cache holds stale bytes, and on ordinary layers the rank rows are quantized from f32 shards, not the canonical bytes. The ON arm failed the step37 serve gate, so set it only with its own receipts. `=1` is the rollback seam for the off arm. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_kv_restore) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_QKV_FUSED` | off | Strict `0`/`1`: inside the v2 decode driver (`MEMRA_STEP_TP_DECODE_V2=1`), one launch per rank computes the three rank-local QKV projections (`matvec_f32_qkv`, block-per-row, f32 accumulate, deterministic tree reduce) instead of about 12 chunked cuBLASLt calls. Numeric class (per-row reduction order); acceptance is the run-gen argmax gate plus the 12-boot battery. Also a prerequisite of `MEMRA_STEP_TP_BATCH` and the dcw path. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_qkv_fused) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8` | step37 (SlidingGatedMoe) models: on; every other model: off | Tri-state: `1` forces on for any family, `0` kills, unset takes the calling model's step37 default. q8_0 mirror of the step TP attention projections for decode: at load each resident bf16 rank shard gets a q8_0 twin, and decode QKV and o_proj run one-launch q8_0 kernels (`qmatvec_q8_0_qkv_rp`); the spec verify walk, and the prime that rides it, use the t-row twins (`qmatvec_q8_0_qkv_rp_t`, `_b4_rp_t`). Gate rows stay bf16 and the bf16 slabs stay resident. Numeric class (weight precision): acceptance is the run-gen argmax gate with its logit maxdiff plus a quality battery. Mirrors cost about 0.9 GB per card; at the natural 262144 context the full W8 set fits only with `MEMRA_SWA_RING=1`. | [KERNELS.md](KERNELS.md#cuqmatveccu), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_w8) |
| `MEMRA_TCOL_FFN` | on for the step37 SlidingGatedMoe program (family-armed); off elsewhere | Only implies the `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ` defer: `0` kills it, `1` forces it for any family. Its named feature, the two-column device-routed MoE FFN sweep for the verify walk, was removed with the `MEMRA_NVFP4_BANK_V2` banks it required. It is not folded into `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ` because recipes that set only this flag would then lose the o_proj defer (`spec.rs`). | [step37-bankv2-removal-20260829](../research/step37-bankv2-removal-20260829/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_tcol_ffn) |
| `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ` / `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ_REF` / `MEMRA_TCOL_PROF` | unset (off); the step37 `MEMRA_TCOL_FFN` default implies the `OPROJ` defer | T-column verify-walk levers. `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ=1` defers each column's o_proj into one batched b4_tcol plus one slab join per layer, bit-exact per column; `MEMRA_TCOL_OPROJ_REF=1` fills the partial slab with the t=1 b4 kernel per column, the choreography-vs-kernel bisect twin; `MEMRA_TCOL_PROF=1` prints a synchronized per-segment wall split of the walk. The syncs serialize the stream, so `PROF` is never a perf claim (`tp.rs`, `spec.rs`). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_tcol_oproj) |
| `MEMRA_W8_HYBRID` | step37 models: on; every other model: off | Tri-state step37 door (`1` forces, `0` kills): the hybrid half of `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8`, q8_0 mirrors for the LM head, shared expert and dense FFN GEMVs, built on first decode use through a pointer-keyed mirror cache (`matvec_bf16_via_q8_mirror`). Engages only inside a step37 model's own launches, never another family's GEMVs. Costs about 1.7 GB per card on top of the attention mirrors; at the natural 262144 context it fits only with `MEMRA_SWA_RING=1`. Same numeric class and argmax acceptance as `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8`; `MEMRA_W8_TRACE` shows whether it fired. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_w8_hybrid) |
| `MEMRA_W8_VIEW` | off | `1` extends the W8 hybrid half to bf16 GEMVs whose weight is a row-range view of a resident slab, the lo halves that `MEMRA_HEAD_SPLIT` and `MEMRA_SHEXP_OVERLAP` keep on rank 0 (`matvec_bf16_view_via_q8_mirror`). Engages only with `MEMRA_STEP_TP_W8` and `MEMRA_W8_HYBRID` on, and prints `[w8-view] mirror built ...` once per shape. Inert on the served step37 route: its decode and verify walks never call the view path, only `run-gen`'s host paths do. Not a step37 family door. Unset or `0` is the rollback seam. The `(ptr, in_f, out_f)` mirror-cache key it brought applies to every W8 mirror. | [step37-decode-headroom-20260828](../research/step37-decode-headroom-20260828/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_w8_view) |

### Gemma

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH` | budget-keyed: graph at `max_new >= 256` within the window, else eager-dc | Gemma E4B whole-token graph-exec-update serving (`graph_update.rs`): one captured step, with per-token FA geometry matched to the live eager split counts; it engages only while the request stays inside the sliding window. `=1` and `=0` force the graph and eager arms; `gemma-gate` (`MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH_GATE`) sets them in-process, so the flag stays. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_e4b_graph) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA4_BATCH=0` | on (batched gemma4 dense-31B decode) | `=0` is the rollback to eager per-session gemma4 dense-31B decode. Unset or `1` is the batched arm; any other value panics at first use, so a mistyped kill switch cannot silently pick a path. Exactness battery covers B=4/8 and batched output is byte-exact against eager. Read once per process. | [SERVED-AGGREGATE.md](../research/gemma-vision-20260816/SERVED-AGGREGATE.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gemma4_batch) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_CSR=0` | on (CSR owner-scan dedup at verify `t <= 10`) | `=0` streams duplicate expert weights at gemma spec verify (`t <= 10`) instead of the owner-scan CSR dedup: the gemma twin of `MEMRA_MOE_CSR`, without its `=2` byte-compare mode. The CSR twin runs only when the gate and up banks are Q4_0, IQ4_XS or IQ3_S; a k-quant bank always takes the rows twin. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gemma_csr) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_MOE_FAST=0` | on (fused gate_up-GELU plus down8 FMA chain) | `=0` sends gemma MoE decode and verify (t below the prime floor) back to the per-expert f32 `qmatvec` loop instead of the fused gate_up-GELU plus down8 FMA chain over the expert pointer table. Verify runs the exact decode kernel chain per token. Rollback for both placements, resident slab and SLRU. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gemma_moe_fast) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_MOE_MMA=0` | on (int8-MMA expert GEMM where eligible) | `=0` runs gemma expert gate, up and down prefill on decode-once dp4a instead of the int8-MMA expert GEMM. The MMA arm needs `n_embd % 256 == 0` and Q4_0, IQ4_XS or IQ3_S for all three banks; a k-quant bank (Q3_K, Q4_K, Q6_K) takes dp4a whatever this flag says. The down GEMM pads its ragged 704 k to whole 256-value superblocks and reads 36 B past an expert's last row, which the zeroed 144 B slab and SLRU slot tails cover. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gemma_moe_mma) |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_MOE_PAIRS=0` | on (pair-batched expert prefill) | `=0` runs the per-expert loop for gemma real prefill (t at or above the prime floor) instead of pair-batched launches over the expert pointer table (resident slab or SLRU). The gemma twin of `MEMRA_MOE_PAIRS`. |  |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_ROWS_W` | on (`=0` reverts) | Gemma decode and verify attention run the parity-law rows/rows_w twins (hd512 globals and hd256 windowed), so decode and verify share one kernel. `=0` takes the per-token loop. |  |

### Qwen, Hyper and Ornith

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `set_verify_mt` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam, no env) | on (`VERIFY_MT_DEFAULT`) | MTP spec verify chunks with 2<=t<=12 run the weight-shared multi-token kernels: `qmatvec_bf16w_mt_f32` on every trunk linear, the hc-diet MT stages on the read gates, and the MoE verify-column merge (one grouped launch per projection per layer). Each per-(row, token) chain is the t=1 decode program verbatim, so rows are bit-identical to per-token launches. Plain decode and big prefills are untouched. OFF arm: `--vmt off` on `qwen4exp_real_gate`. | [MTP-SPEC.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/MTP-SPEC.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_verify_mt) |
| Card-1 draft placement (qwen4exp_gpu `load_from_dir_dev1` / `from_loaded_checkpoint_dual`; gate binary `--mtp-dev1`) | off in the gate binary (`--mtp-dev1` arms it); the recommended spec placement | The MTP draft block (weights plus the DeviceBf16 expert bank) and a private same-bytes copy of the shared LM head build on card 1, with draft state and workspace there; the spec loop crosses the seed rows over P2P each round (`memcpy_peer_async`) and drafted ids keep their 4-byte copy. Draft-engine identity is enforced (`check_draft_engine`). A prerequisite past about 400 prompt tokens, where a co-resident draft runs out of memory. Rollback: drop the flag. | [mtp10](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp10/), [PROFILE-7.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-7.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#load_from_dir_dev1) |
| FR-Spec draft-head trim (qwen4exp_gpu `build_draft_trim`; `--draft-trim <ranks.txt> [--draft-trim-n N]`, `--trim-sweep`, `--trim-ab`) | off (full-vocab draft head) | The MTP draft scores only a top-N own-generation rank subset of the vocab, rows gathered device to device from the shared LM head, so a trimmed row's logit equals its full-vocab twin; the target verify stays full-vocab, so committed output cannot move, only acceptance. Armed only by naming a ranks file built by `--owngen` from this artifact's own generations, never inferred. Arms: `--trim-ab` (interleaved, fails on any chain divergence); width table: `--trim-sweep`. | [mtp9](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp9/), [PROFILE-6.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-6.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#build_draft_trim) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_EP_MAP` | unset (even split) | Path to a `memra-ep-map-v1` JSON (minted by `tools/build_expert_placement_map.py` from `MEMRA_MOE_TRACE` route traces) assigning every expert to card 0 or 1 per layer for qwen4_exp TP2, the same frozen format `MEMRA_GLM5_EP_MAP` reads. One resolved placement per layer rides the shard and drives both the card-1 bank upload and the route splits, so they cannot disagree. Unset, empty or `0` is the even split `rank = expert / (experts/2)`, bit-identical to the pre-seam engine. Refuses on a wrong `format`, `ranks` != 2, a mismatched `expert_count`, an uncovered MoE layer, a rank outside {0,1} or an unbalanced layer (card 1 must own exactly `experts/2`). | [ROUND2-STATUS.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/kvq/ROUND2-STATUS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_ep_map) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_IDX_SCORE_CAP_MF` | 32 (`32 << 20` floats = 128 MB) | Block-score slab cap for `indexer_select_rows`, in mega-floats: bounds the row sub-batch (`cap / complete_blocks` rows) whose scores are computed on device and copied back before the host top-k. A value that does not parse, or 0, keeps 32. It moves only the batching of a device launch, never scores or selected sets, so it is not a correctness seam. | [LADDER.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/round2-box-receipts/LADDER.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_idx_score_cap_mf) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_PEER_KV_MAX_CAP` | 8192 rows | Row ceiling for a peer-resident QSA KV state (`alloc_state_reserve` with a `kv_engine` on another card, i.e. `qwen4exp_real_gate --ladder-kv-dev1`). Past it the engine at state allocation and the ladder CLI at argument parse both refuse by name, with the mechanism and the route that works. The block-list reader is a scatter reader whose redundancy the local L2 absorbs; peer memory is not cached there, so each selected position becomes a PCIe round trip. Put the MTP draft state on card 1 (`--mtp-dev1`) instead. Raise it only to re-measure the cliff; it changes no arithmetic. | [HANG.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/kvdev1-hang/HANG.md), [YARN-CELL.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/yarn/YARN-CELL.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_peer_kv_max_cap) |
| `set_idx_cache` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxcache=0` disables) | on (`IDX_CACHE_DEFAULT`) | Device-resident indexer raw-key cache. Below the QSA selection horizon the per-layer idx_proj device-to-host copy only fed the host raw-key cache; this seam appends the k-part rows device to device (`copy_rows_col_f32`, exact byte moves) and materializes the host cache lazily at the first scored chunk, so the scored path is bit-identical by construction. Mid-run flips backfill in both directions; rewind clamps the device row count with the host truncation. The TP2 indexer keeps its host twin and pays the host-cache debt on entry. Decided with `routerdev` as one stack. Rollback: `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxcache=0`. | [devtwin](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/devtwin/), [PROFILE-9.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-9.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_idx_dev` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxdev=0` disables) | on (`IDX_DEV` init true) | The qwen4_exp QSA indexer scores pooled micro-block keys on the GPU (`qsa_index_score_f32`, explicit `__fmul_rn`/`__fadd_rn`/`__fdiv_rn` so the dim loop is not contracted into FMA) instead of the host twin. Scores are bit-identical to the host twin, which stays the reference and the TP2 path. The host twin's cost is O(context) per token per layer. Rollback: `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxdev=0` (pure host scorer). | [yarn](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/yarn/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_idx_sel` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxsel=0` disables) | on (`IDX_SEL_DEFAULT`) | Device QSA indexer top-k selection (`qsa_index_topk_u32`): selection runs on device and reads back `rows x budget` u32 instead of the `rows x blocks` f32 score slab, removing the host top-k that dominated deep prefill chunks. Exact by construction: the sort key packs `~f32_total_asc_u32(score)` above `block_index`, so its ascending order equals the host `sel_cmp` (score descending, block index ascending) over the whole f32 domain, and keys are distinct. Needs no pairing with other seams. Rollback: `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxsel=0`; live audit: `MEMRA_Q4E_IDXSEL_AUDIT=1`. | [PROFILE-11.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-11.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_idxq` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxq`) | q8 (`IDXQ_MODE` init 1) | Indexer raw-key cache precision: the 128-dim pre-norm, pre-rope keys (host cache and the idxcache device cache) store as q8_0 or bf16 rows instead of f32 (512 to 136 or 256 B per token per QSA layer) and are dequantized at read, so output can move only through the selection set. Host and device quantize twins are bit-matching. Latched per state at alloc. `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=idxq` arms q8, `idxq=bf16` bf16, `idxq=0` or `idxq=f32` (or a valueless OFF) restores f32; other values are refused and apply nothing; save and restore go through `seam_value`. Selection identity: `MEMRA_Q4E_IDXQ_AUDIT=1`. | [kvq](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/kvq/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_kv_quant` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=kvq`) | on (`KV_QUANT_DEFAULT`: K q8_0, V q5_1) | Quantized QSA KV cache: K rows q8_0 (34 B per 32), V rows q5_1 (24 B per 32), about 11.1 KiB per token against 49 KiB for f32. Storage only: attention runs f32 on dequantized values (`q4e_sdpa_blocklist_q8q5`, bit-identical to dequant then `sdpa_blocklist_f32`), and a quantized state always takes the block-list path. The format latches per state at `alloc_state`/`mtp_state`. Kept on for memory (it lifts the single-card context ceiling about 4.4x); it is slower at depth, so do not quote it as faster there. `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=kvq` arms; rollback `kvq=0` (new states allocate f32). | [kvq](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/kvq/), [LADDER.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/round2-box-receipts/LADDER.md), [PROFILE-10.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-10.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_kv_quant), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_longatt` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=longatt`) | auto (`LONGATT_MODE` init 0) | Long-context QSA attention form: `sdpa_blocklist_f32` over each row's own bounded position list (at most 2052 entries on real geometry) instead of a dense [t, t_kv] mask. Auto engages when the block-list form reads strictly fewer KV rows (any non-full row) and always past t_kv 12288, the masked kernel's shared-memory bound; below the drop point auto keeps the masked path. The forced arm is bit-identical to the masked kernel on the same selection. `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=longatt` forces, `longatt=0` forbids, `longatt=auto` restores auto; other values are refused and apply nothing. | [yarn](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/yarn/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_ple_cache` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=plecache`) | on (`PLE_CACHE_DEFAULT`) | Incremental PLE n-gram id cache: `ple_block` appends to a per-state id cache instead of recomputing `host_ngram_ids` over the full token history every step (O(context) per decode token). Exact by construction: `ids[token]` depends only on `token_ids[..=token]`, so appended rows never change; rewind and divergence use a longest-common-prefix compare, never a length compare. Costs one i64 id vector per state in host memory (`fill*16*8` bytes), no device memory. Live truth pin: `MEMRA_Q4E_PLECACHE_AUDIT=1`. Rollback: `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=plecache=0`. | [PROFILE-12.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-12.md), [PROFILE-11.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-11.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_router_dev` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=routerdev=0` disables) | on (`ROUTER_DEV_DEFAULT`) | Device MoE router: `qwen4exp_route_topk_f32` runs the full `host_route_softmax_topk` program on device (softmax over 512 logits in host op order, top-10 under the pinned tie rule, renorm floor 6.1035156e-5) and writes selections and weights into the grouped NVFP4 dispatch slots, removing the blocking router copies. Engages on grouped NVFP4 paths only; the per-expert prefill executor, the DeviceBf16 draft bank and the TP2 route keep the host twin. Contract: selection ids and order exact, weights ULP-bounded, checked live by `MEMRA_Q4E_ROUTER_AUDIT=1`. Pair it with `idxcache`: alone with decode graphs on it lost. Rollback: `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=routerdev=0`. | [devtwin](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/devtwin/), [PROFILE-9.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-9.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_sel_group` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam; `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS=selgroup`) | on: auto for both families (`SEL_GROUP_AUTO`) | Sub-warp pair groups for the qwen4_exp NVFP4 MoE sel matvecs: `qmatvec_nvfp4_modelopt_sel_g_f32` and `..._gu_silu_g_f32` replace the shipped kernels, whose 32-lane pair loop leaves lanes idle here (down 20 pairs, gate+up 80). `g` lanes share an output row and a warp carries `32/g` groups of `rows` rows; auto is `rows = 4` with `g` the largest power of two dividing the pair count (down 4, gate+up 16), falling back to the shipped kernel when `out_f` does not tile. Accumulation class, not bit-identical, except `g=32,rows=4`. `selgroup` arms auto, `selgroup=dn:4:4+gu:16:4` pins arms, `selgroup=0` rolls back; an omitted name means auto, so pin an A/B's off arm. Gate: `gate_nvfp4_sel_group`. | [downsel](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/downsel/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_sel_group), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `MEMRA_QWEN_DC` | on (`=0` reverts) | Qwen greedy serving runs the dc-eager loop: device argmax and device counters, about 4 bytes per token of host traffic. `=0` falls back to the host-logits eager loop (full-vocab DtoH and host argmax per token). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_qwen_dc) |
| qwen4_exp defer sequential-guard sub-arm (`SpecOpts::defer_guard_sync`; gate binary `--spec-defer-guard-sync`) | off | With `defer` and `pmin`, keeps the guard's chain stop sequential (one 4-byte probability copy per chain step, stopping exactly at the sub-threshold step) instead of reading the whole confidence window at the drain and truncating afterwards. Same picks and counters bit for bit; the default deferred guard dispatches the full window, so collapsed stretches pay drafts this arm never runs. `--defer-ab` runs host, defer and defer-gsync interleaved when pmin is armed. | [mtp11](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp11/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#specoptsdefer_guard_sync) |
| qwen4_exp deferred round readback (`SpecOpts::defer`; gate binary `--spec-defer`, A/B `--defer-ab <reps>x<tokens>`) | off | Ports spec.rs's deferred round readback (`MEMRA_DSPARK_DEFER_READBACK`) to the qwen4_exp round: the draft chain's argmax feeds the next step on device (`argmax_token_device_col` and a draft-engine-resident embed table armed by `arm_spec_devchain`), guard confidences land in device slots, and the round drains once before the verify; t=1 steps commit by device argmax and the prefill copies back one row. The floor is a 2-drain round, since the PLE block hashes the chunk's actual token ids on the host. Bytes identical by construction; `--defer-ab` fails on any chain or admission-counter divergence. Refuses with `--spec-trace` and on a trim-state mismatch. Rollback: drop the flag. | [mtp11](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp11/), [PROFILE-8.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-8.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#specoptsdefer) |
| qwen4_exp dyn-K decay (`SpecOpts::dynk`; gate binary `--spec-dynk <window>,<thr>,<k_floor>`) | off | Rolling-window K decay for qwen4_exp spec; floor 0 turns spec off for the rest of the generation (plain host-argmax tail). Built as the bounded fallback and not part of the recommended admission policy, which held every measured cell without it. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#specoptsdynk) |
| qwen4_exp spec admission, p-min draft guard + adaptive K (`SpecOpts`; gate binary `--spec-pmin <p>`, `--spec-adapt <k_lo>`) | off in the gate binary; recommended `--spec-adapt 1 --spec-pmin 0.3` | p-min guard with `MEMRA_SPEC_PMIN` semantics (a sub-threshold draft token is discarded uncounted; a fully guarded round verifies t=1, one plain step that still commits) plus adaptive K with `MEMRA_DFLASH_ADAPT` semantics (next k = clamp(accepted+1, k_lo, K)). Confidence comes from the shared `prob_of_token` kernels plus a 4-byte copy per draft step. Both knobs only shrink the drafted window and commits are always target rows, so output is byte-identical by construction. Rollback: drop the flags (fixed-K program). | [mtp10](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp10/), [PROFILE-7.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-7.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#specopts) |

### Other families

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_LOCKSTEP_GROUPED` | unset (auto: grouped from m >= 3) | Hy3 multi-stream lockstep decode, MoE layers. `1` forces grouped (all m rows through `moe_ffn_lockstep`, resident experts amortize weight reads; CPU-assigned experts keep per-row companion calls), `0` forces per-row, unset groups from m >= 3. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_lockstep_grouped) |

## Hardware-keyed knobs

Switches that exist for one build target, Hopper (`sm_90a`) or B200 (`sm_100a`); a row whose default only differs by card stays in its subsystem's table with the device key in its Default cell. The arch is detected from the first GPU (`MEMRA_CUDA_ARCH` forces it), each promoted Hopper default reverts with `=0`, and on `sm_100a` the B200 matvec occupancy arms run by default with no switch, each bit-identical per output. The Hopper evidence ledger is [ARCHITECTURE-H100.md](../ARCHITECTURE-H100.md); GB10 defaults key on unified memory and are listed in [SERVING.md](SERVING.md#unified-memory-devices-gb10-dgx-spark).

### Hopper (sm_90a)

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_FA3` | ON on sm_90a builds; OFF elsewhere | FA3 full-attention prefill (TMA swizzled Q/K/V ring plus wgmma) for head_dim 256 causal self-attention. `=1` on any other build refuses at `Engine::new` (boot) and again at the switch, since `fa3_prefill.cu` is a stub there. `=0` reverts. |  |
| `MEMRA_GDN_WGMMA` | ON on sm_90a builds (inside the mma config); never taken elsewhere | Fused K4+K5 wgmma kernel (`gdn_k45_wgmma`) plus K2 on wgmma, with bf16 q/P mirrors; Y and Ssnap are never materialized. Nested inside `MEMRA_GDN_MMA`. The wgmma bodies exist only in sm_90a builds (`MEMRA_K45_REAL`), so other builds never take the arm. `=0` reverts. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gdn_wgmma) |
| `MEMRA_WGMMA=1` | unset (MMQ) | `1` uses the hand-rolled wgmma GEMM v0 (unpipelined 64x64 tile, `wait_group<0>` every 32-K step) for q8_0 prefill instead of MMQ, on Hopper MMA builds only (`cfg!(memra_hopper_mma)`). Slower than MMQ per launch at m=512 model shapes; MMQ stays the default until a pipelined version wins N=5. Kernel-check's wgmma case is cfg-gated, not env-gated (`lib.rs wgmma_gemm_enabled()`). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_wgmma) |

### B200 (sm_100a)

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_B200_BF16_GEMV_LT` | off | `1` routes the bf16 decode row matvec (`matvec_bf16_rows_into`, the `matvec_bf16_f32acc_x4_rows` kernel) through `cublasLtMatmul` at m=t. A measurement instrument, never a serving arm: numeric class `bf16_gemv_lt` casts the activation to bf16 and sums K in the library's order, so it is not bit-identical. Compiled for `sm_100a` builds only; a no-op elsewhere. `MEMRA_KDA_FUSED_PROJ`'s bf16 arm declines while it is on; a cuBLASLt refusal sends the shape back to the shipped kernel, and engagement and refusal announce once per shape. `MEMRA_B200_GEMV_V2` takes precedence on the bf16 rows. Rollback: unset. | [LANE.md](../research/b200-gemv-hbm-20260902/LANE.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_bf16_gemv_lt) |
| `MEMRA_B200_DSA_DECODE` | `100a` builds: `2`; other builds compiled out (`0`) | sm_100a t<=8 MLA/DSA decode door. `1`: bit-identical arms only, the single-pass gathered kernel and the (head, pool)-blocked k-pool scorer. `2` adds the warp-online gathered arm, named numeric class `dsa-warp-online-f32`, argmax-gated and admitted only at t_q=1 (`mla_dsa_attn_arm_effective` demotes it above). The scorer engages from 4096 pools. A refused geometry falls through to the shipped path. `0` is the rollback, any other value reads as `2`; read per call. Gate `dsa-decode-gate`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_dsa_decode), [ROOFLINE.md](../research/b200-dsa-decode-20260902/ROOFLINE.md) |
| `MEMRA_B200_DSA_SELECT` | `100a` builds: on; other builds compiled out | Exact multi-CTA DSA k-pool selection on sm_100a, replacing the one-CTA-per-token `memra_mla_kpool_select_kernel`: six launches reproduce the `select_k`-th smallest 64-bit order key, so the selection matches bit for bit. Engages at t_q <= 8 above a pool floor keyed on t_q: 65536 pools (262,144 tokens exactly) at t_q = 1, 262144 pools at t_q >= 2, so a prompt called 256k is usually under the floor. Composes with `MEMRA_B200_DSA_DECODE` and `MEMRA_GLM5_DECODE_GRAPH`. Only `=0` disables; read per call. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_dsa_select), [LANE.md](../research/b200-dsa-select-20260903/LANE.md) |
| `MEMRA_B200_GEMV_V2` | off; decide-by: 2026-11-17 | Strict `1` arms the v2 decode matvec family on `sm_100a` builds (a no-op elsewhere; any other value, `2` included, is off): the bf16 rows (`matvec_bf16_v2` and its split-K pair, `qmatvec_kda6_bf16f32_v2`) and the W8-posture q8_0 family (`qmatvec_q8_0_mmvq_rp_v2` at t=1, `qmatvec_q8_0_rows_tw_v2` at the t<=8 verify width, and the fused `qmatvec_kda6_q8f32_rp_v2`, which also needs `MEMRA_KDA_FUSED_PROJ`). Every arm is bit-identical per output except the split-K class. Takes precedence over `MEMRA_B200_BF16_GEMV_LT` on the bf16 rows. Read once per process. Owner's call by the date: promote `=1` to the sm_100a default, or delete it with every kernel only it reaches. | [GLM5-B200-DOORS.md](decisions/GLM5-B200-DOORS.md), [LANE.md](../research/b200-gemv-hbm-20260902/LANE.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_gemv_v2) |
| `MEMRA_B200_MR1_FILL=<n>` | `16` (`sm_100a` builds) | Threshold of the B200 grid-fill arm inside the sm_100a matvec occupancy arms: the NVFP4 m=1 decode matvec forces mr1 (one warp per row, double the grid, the shipped `qmatvec_nvfp4_mmvq_rp`, byte-identical per row) when the mr2 grid would be fewer than `n` four-warp blocks per SM. `16` is one full wave. A value below 1 or unparseable keeps 16. No effect on other builds. | [GLM5-B200-DOORS.md](decisions/GLM5-B200-DOORS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_b200_mr1_fill) |
| `MEMRA_DSA_SCORE_RP` | `1` | Pools per thread of the sm_100a decode k-pool scorer (`memra_mla_kpool_score_dsa_kernel`). Only `=2` selects the earlier two-pool shape, the rollback; anything else is 1, which doubles the working grid. Bit-identical either way: each thread accumulates in the same order and the head mix stays in one thread. Latched once per process. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_dsa_score_rp) |
| `MEMRA_Q8_ROW_ILP` | on for `sm_100a` builds, off elsewhere; engages only under `MEMRA_B200_GEMV_V2=1` | A sub-arm of `MEMRA_B200_GEMV_V2`: unset follows the build arch (on for `100a`), `1`/`0` override, read per call. Under that door the W8-posture q8_0 row walk takes its `_ilp` twins (`qmatvec_q8_0_mmvq_rp_v2_ilp` at t=1, `qmatvec_kda6_q8f32_rp_v2_ilp` for the fused six-projection group): four blocks' loads per lane issue ahead of the dp4a chains. Bit-identical by construction (gate `b200_matvec_bench` families 7 and 9). Announces `[q8-row-ilp] engaged`. A naked sm_100a run never reaches it, because the parent door is off by default. | [GLM5-B200-DOORS.md](decisions/GLM5-B200-DOORS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q8_row_ilp) |

## Diagnostics and tracing

Dumps, traces, probes, timing logs and debug switches for localizing a defect or attributing time. Almost all are off by default; a row says when one adds synchronization, copies or a different code path, so times taken under it are not serving numbers and none belongs in a serving launch. The kernels these probes name are in [KERNELS.md](KERNELS.md).

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| `MEMRA_TP2_*` | unset; booleans off, `_T` 4096, `_QT` 7 (v1), `_LAYERS` 1, `_COPY_ROWS` 32, `_ROOT_BUSY` 0 | Inputs of the standalone `moe-tp2-repro` bin (`crates/memra-engine/src/bin/moe_tp2_repro.rs`), a TP2 harness for the grouped NVFP4 MoE; the server reads none. `MEMRA_TP2_RANDBANK=1` fills the expert bank with random bytes (a zero bank makes a determinism verdict vacuous; an all-zero comparison is tagged `VACUOUS`). `MEMRA_TP2_T` sets the token count, `MEMRA_TP2_QT` the bank layout (7 = v1, 107 = v2). `MEMRA_TP2_DETERMINISM=1` runs the MoE twice and diffs; `MEMRA_TP2_DETERM_CONCURRENT=1` keeps rank 1 busy; `MEMRA_TP2_LAYERS` repeats the layer. `MEMRA_TP2_PEERCOPY` / `_COPY_ROWS` / `_COPY_FIRST` / `_ROOT_BUSY` / `_FRESH_ALLOC` are the cross-rank copy and allocation arms. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_tp2_randbank) |
| `MEMRA_ALLOC_SITES=1` | unset | `1` prints both call sites for every engine allocation whose block was last handed out on a different stream (`[alloc-sites] XSTREAM reuse ptr=.. now file:line .. <- last file:line ..`, first 24 lines), naming the main-stream owner a side-stream allocation lands on. One hash-map lock per allocation; debug only (`lib.rs alloc_uninit`, `#[track_caller]` through the `uninit*` wrappers). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_alloc_sites) |
| `MEMRA_ALLOC_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | Gate-harness instrument, never a serving flag. Every Engine device allocation funnel (`alloc_uninit<T>`, `zeros`, `alloc_u8`, `alloc_u8_uninit`, `alloc_u32_zeroed`, the typed `uninit_*` wrappers) and host upload (`htod`, `htod_i32`, `htod_i8`, `htod_u64`, `htod_bytes`) prints `[alloc-trace] <bytes> bytes from <file>:<line>` naming its caller. DFlash adds `[dflash-alloc]` phase snapshots, `[dflash-oracle]` SHA-256 digests of target taps, draft features, positions and logits, and `[dflash-taps]` chunk-sink and carry sizes. Timings taken under it are not performance receipts. Only `1` arms it; storage policy is unchanged. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_alloc_trace) |
| `MEMRA_BATCH_PHASE` | off | `1` adds sync-bounded per-phase accumulators inside `decode_step_batch` (12 phases, printed by `decode-batch-bench`). Shares rank the tick; the added syncs inflate the total. Diagnosis only. |  |
| `MEMRA_BURST_VCHECK=1` | unset (off) | In-place gate harness for the gemma burst verify: runs the verify stream on each eager round's batch before the eager verify and diffs argmaxes and per-layer KV byte sums. State is untouched because the eager append overwrites the same rows. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_CHAIN_PHASE` | unset (off) | `1` alternates token-chain work across phase streams without the cross-token event edges: a timing-ceiling probe whose output deliberately races and is wrong. Never use its tape for correctness or serving (`decode.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_CONFIDENCE_TRACE=<path>` | unset (off) | memra-server: per-token confidence JSONL (`memra-token-confidence-v1`: reference logprob, top-1 margin, entropy). Disables KV reuse, spec and prime batching: a measurement harness mode, never a serving config. It does not cap admission; send one request at a time for a single-session trace. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_confidence_trace) |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG=1` | unset | Prints one-shot engagement receipts (`[memra] ... ENGAGED` to stderr) for fused and batched dispatch arms, keyed per door, so an A/B arm proves in the log that it is live. Read with `is_ok()`: any value, `0` included, turns it on (`lib.rs`, `spec.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_AFFINITY=1` | unset | memra-server logs why a plain-affinity checkpoint capture was skipped, with the checkpoint and prompt ids around the divergence; the session then re-primes in full next turn. Any value turns it on (`worker.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_FAPOOL=1` | unset | Prints FA partials-pool tracing (`[fa-pool] REALLOC/retire` lines), the instrument for the dangling-pool defect class. Any value turns it on (`lib.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_PRIMESEG=1` | unset (off) | memra-server: prints the plain-tier prime call sequence per request, each prime segment's boundary and stop, and every prompt token the W1 tokenwise door sends through `decode_step`, so a cross-prime-path byte comparison knows which program ran. No numeric effect. Any set value arms it. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_debug_primeseg) |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_SPEC=1` | unset (off) | Per-round speculative decode trace on stderr, including turn-checkpoint skips and the gemma round state (with `MEMRA_ROUND_GRAPH_CHECK=1`). |  |
| `MEMRA_DEBUG_ZERO_ALLOCS=1` | unset | `1` memsets every engine allocation (`lib.rs alloc_uninit`): the global uninitialized-read discriminator, covering engine-internal buffers a prime-scoped zeroing cannot reach. Heavy launch overhead; debug only. |  |
| `MEMRA_DFLASH_DEBUG=1` | off | `1` prints per-round DFlash debug lines (first-round block state and similar). |  |
| `MEMRA_DFLASH_DUMP=<dir>` | off | Writes DFlash stage dumps (ctx_features, q/k stages, layer outputs; f32 LE) into `<dir>` for the oracle parity bisect. |  |
| `MEMRA_DFLASH_NOROPE` | off | Set skips rope on the DFlash draft q. Bring-up bisect arm. |  |
| `MEMRA_DRAFT_GRAPH_CHECK=1` | unset (off) | After each gemma draft-chain graph replay, re-runs the chain eagerly and diffs the drafted slots, then restores the graph's tokens so the round proceeds unchanged: a non-destructive replay-versus-eager bisect. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_DSPARK_FULLG_DEBUG=1` | unset (off) | DSpark verify graphs: per-round print of why the full-verify graph did or did not capture or replay (rung, coverage, batchable checks). Only `1` arms it; diagnostic only. |  |
| `MEMRA_DSV4_MOE_M1_STREAM`, `MEMRA_DSV4_MOE_MROW_STREAM`, `MEMRA_TIER_RECEIPT_FATBIN` | no reader: setting one changes nothing | Names the catalog still lists with no reader in the code, so the boot audit keeps accepting them. `MEMRA_DSV4_MOE_M1_STREAM` and `MEMRA_DSV4_MOE_MROW_STREAM` were lane-only reads that measured the DSV4 one-token and 2..=16-row MoE stream visitors (`moe_kq_m1_stream_kernel<4>`, `moe_kq_mrow_stream_kernel<4>`); neither read merged, both visitors are the naked default, rollback is `git revert`, and `set_dsv4_moe_m1_stream_for_gate(false)` runs the sktail reference arm for both. `MEMRA_TIER_RECEIPT_FATBIN` was the fatbin path of the host-tier receipt kernels, which went with the host-tier contracts door (see Removed doors). | [RESULTS.md](../research/dsv4f-bringup-20260923/m1-stream-664/RESULTS.md), [RESULTS.md](../research/dsv4f-bringup-20260923/mrow-stream/RESULTS.md), [FLAGS-HISTORY.md](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#catalog-notes-before-the-2026-11-12-rewrite) |
| `MEMRA_DSV4_NVTX` | unset (off) | `1` emits NVTX push/pop ranges for the DSV4 phases and the full-token replay token phases, with no added synchronization, so an `nsys profile -t cuda,nvtx` run attributes GPU-busy time per range; unset emits no NVTX calls and no timing accumulation. A build without the nvtx3 header turns the ranges into no-ops. `dsv4_tp_ep_sampled_perf_gate --full-token-replay-profile` requires it and is never a scored arm; `dsv4_launch_boundary_gate` requires it unset. | [FLAGS-HISTORY.md](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#catalog-notes-before-the-2026-11-12-rewrite) |
| `MEMRA_DTOH_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | Gate-harness instrument, never a serving flag. Every Engine device-to-host wrapper (`dtoh`, `dtoh_i32`, `dtoh_*_one`, the views, pairs and pinned variants) prints `[dtoh-trace] <bytes> bytes from <file>:<line>` naming its caller (`#[track_caller]`), one line per copy, so a blocking readback can be removed at its source. Only `1` arms it; off costs one OnceLock read per copy. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_dtoh_trace) |
| `MEMRA_DUMP_HN=<path>` | unset (off) | Appends pre-head hidden states per decode step for head-MIPS feasibility and offline bound analysis. Both decode arms share the door so they stay observably identical. Adds a DtoH per step; diagnostic only. |  |
| `MEMRA_E4B_DCG_EAGER` | unset (capture and replay) | E4B door bisect arm: runs the dcg step eagerly per token instead of capture and replay. `2` uses the capture's fixed win bucket; `1` (or any other set value) the exact live bucket. Separates bucket-path numerics from the replay mechanism. |  |
| `MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH_TIMING=1` | unset (off) | Per-phase host timing of the E4B graph loop: dtoh-wait, `fa_apply` and launch sums. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_EXACT16_WHY=1` | unset (off, no cost) | Prints why a checkpoint is refused the exact-16 decode-batch tier. `decode_batch_exact16_ok` is an all over every matmul, so one class without a bit-exact b16 kernel silently drops the whole checkpoint to chunk 8. Prints `[exact16] REFUSED by L<n>.<tensor> qtype=<q> rp4=<bool>` per refusing tensor, plus the structural architecture, MLA-mixer and MoE-ffn refusals that can never be admitted. Any set value arms it. | [rp-on-st-20260806](../research/rp-on-st-20260806/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_exact16_why) |
| `MEMRA_F16G_DEBUG=1` | unset (off) | Stage bisect for the grouped f16 expert lane: full NaN/Inf scans of w, act and y plus post-permute and post-scatter bad-value counts, to localize a corrupt stage. Adds DtoH syncs; diagnosis only. Any set value arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_FA_PART_ZERO` | unset (off) | Strict `1`, a discriminator, not a fix: zeroes the three fa partial banks each time the pool grows, turning NaNs from unwritten banks into finite but wrong zeros. Read it one way only: zeroed banks carry `m = 0.0`, not `NEG_INF`, so a wholly unwritten bank still combines to NaN; silence under this arm convicts the pool, continued trapping acquits nothing. Every grow goes through `fa_part_alloc`, which prints an unconditional `[fa-pool] grow` receipt bounded to 64 lines. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_fa_part_zero) |
| `MEMRA_FA_PROF` | off | `1` runs the clock64-instrumented decode-attention walk (`fa_decode_vec_q_v3_dcw_prof`): each block's first-warp leader stamps the walk's phase boundaries and accumulates cycle deltas into an 8-slot buffer, printed as shares every 430 launches. Needs no counter permission, so it works where ncu cannot. Extra atomics per block; never a measured configuration. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_fa_prof) |
| `MEMRA_FORCE_LOGITS_AT` / `MEMRA_FORCE_LOGITS_FILE` | unset | `run-gen`, with `MEMRA_FORCE_TOKENS_FILE`: write to `_FILE` the raw LE f32 logit row that predicts the zero-based forced-tape position `_AT`. Both variables and the tape are required, and `_AT` must lie inside `MEMRA_NGEN`, so an incomplete probe refuses instead of measuring another trajectory. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_force_logits_at) |
| `MEMRA_FORCE_TOKENS_FILE` | unset | `run-gen`: teacher-force numeric token ids from a file so placement A/Bs traverse an identical decode route; also the decode-side exactness instrument (disagreement count plus tape NLL). Pair it with a reverse-tape control, since a tape that is one arm's own greedy output favours that arm. A tape shorter than the decode window refuses. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_force_tokens_file) |
| `MEMRA_G4_PRIME_TRACE=1` | unset | `1` prints the gemma4 prime bring-up trace: layer-0 stage stats (NaN count and amax of the q, attn and moe stages) to stderr (`hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_GDN_DIFF=1` | unset (off) | Dual-run oracle: at `t >= 16` each chunked GDN prefill call is checked against the sequential scan (broadcast heads only). It also turns off the k45 norm route. Any set value arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_GEMMA_PROBE=1` | unset | Gemma bring-up bisect: per-layer trunk stats (host rms and max of the last token row) to compare against llama-eval-callback node stats. Any value turns it on (`hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_GLM5_GRAPH_SEL_LEDGER=1` | unset (off) | Gate-harness instrument for `MEMRA_GLM5_DECODE_GRAPH`, never a serving flag. Records the routed-MoE selection and weights per layer per token on both arms so `glm5-decode-graph-gate` can assert the device-table arm matches the host oracle. Capture-legal, unlike `MEMRA_MOE_TRACE`/`WEIGHT_TRACE` and `hidden_trace`: the device arm copies into a persistent per-(device, layer) slot armed before capture (`memcpy_dtod`, no sync, no DtoH), the gate drains it after the token. Changes no kernel or launch order. Only `1` arms it; read per call. Module `crates/memra-engine/src/glm5_sel_ledger.rs`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_glm5_graph_sel_ledger) |
| `MEMRA_GLM5_GRAPH_SELFCHECK_N` | `1` | Depth of the `MEMRA_GLM5_DECODE_GRAPH` self-check: the first N replays of every captured run are compared bitwise against an eager walk of the same layers on the same state, output and the recurrent state written (conv_state, ssm_state, ssm_state_alt, role parity), and a mismatch latches the stage eager. `N>1` covers both ping-pong phases and names `replay=k/N phase=p`. Never above 1 in serving: each verified replay costs an eager step. Values below 1 or unparsable read as 1. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_glm5_graph_selfcheck_n) |
| `MEMRA_GLM5_GRAPH_TRACE` | unset (off) | Gate-harness trace, never a serving flag (`glm5-decode-graph-gate --trace`): strict `=1` prints one `[glm5-graph-trace] pos=P dev=N stage=[lo,hi) seg=[a,b) arm=... sum=0x... nz=K/W absmax=...` line per captured-run boundary per token on both arms, at the same layer boundaries (the eager arm splits its loop at the same cuts, same kernels and order). `nz=` tells an all-zero hidden from a wrong one. Costs one DtoH per segment per token. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_glm5_graph_trace) |
| `MEMRA_GLM5_SPEC_TRACE` | off | Family alias of `MEMRA_SPEC_TRACE`: same values and resolver (`spec_phase.rs`), honored alone. When both names are set to different levels the general name wins with one loud `[spec-trace]` line. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_glm5_spec_trace) |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_CENSUS=1` | unset (off) | Prints the captured graph's node-type census (kernel, memset, memcpy, alloc and free counts) when it is instantiated, on the decode, prime and DSpark verify graph paths (`[dspark-vg-census]` lines there). Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_NODES_DUMP=1` | unset (off) | At capture, dumps the E4B graph's kernel-node inventory (symbol, grid, count) and its FA update-unit count. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_GRAPH_STATS=1` | unset | Engine, gemma graph door: any value prints `[gemma-graph] captures=/buckets=` at the end of the graph loop (`hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_GV_FORCE_CAUSAL=1` | unset | Wrong-arm probe seam: `1` splices the vision overlay rows but keeps the plain causal mask, so the probe can show that the island mask itself changes the answer. `gemma-vision-e2e` sets it under `MEMRA_GV_CAUSAL=1`. Never on in serving (`hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_HYPER_TRACE=<path>` | unset (off by design) | Writes a `memra-hidden-trace-v1` trace of the hyper-connection (mHC) trunk from both `memra-reference` and the CUDA `forward_hyper` walk, so the two diff stage by stage on the same token ids. One line per stage per layer (`expand`, `mixer`, `attn`, MoE `router`/`route`/`routed`, `ffn`, `layer`, `collapse`), last token row only. Stateless prefill walk only; `prime_cache_hyper` and `decode_step_hyper` carry no taps. Unlike the `MEMRA_MOE_*` traces it changes no dispatch, but it costs a `dtoh` per stage and tens of MB per run: never on in a measured or gated run. | [glm53-flash-bringup-20260827](../research/glm53-flash-bringup-20260827/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_hyper_trace) |
| `MEMRA_KDA_STEP_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | Gate-harness instrument, never a serving flag. After each KDA core sub-step (`proj`, `conv+l2`, `gates`, `scan`, `gated_norm`) prints `[kda-step-trace] t=<t> <stage>: name=<non-finite>/<len> ...`, one DtoH per buffer, so a step that returns NaN from a finite input names its kernel. Only `1` arms it; off costs one OnceLock read per sub-step. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_kda_step_trace) |
| `MEMRA_KV_HOST_VERIFY` | `0` (off) | `1` digests the device entry at demote, stores the digest with the host entry and re-checks it at promote: non-GLM entries use `split-state-v3:<hex>` (trunk planes, conv/ssm, latent, MTP draft window, boundary hidden and logits, DFlash tail), GLM entries `host-prefix-state-v1:<hex>`. A mismatch, or a carried digest from another program, logs `[prefix-host] VERIFY FAILED`, drops the host entry, counts `digest_mismatches` and serves cold. Gate and diagnostic arm only: it D2Hs every plane byte on both sides. `tools/kv-host-spill-failure-gate.sh` covers both arms. Digests survive the deploy handoff. | [DAY53.md](../research/spill-c-20260919/DAY53.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_kv_host_verify) |
| `MEMRA_KV_PLANE_SCAN` / `MEMRA_KV_PLANE_SCAN_ROUNDS` | off / 2 | Strict `1`: before a global-attention layer runs in the t-column verify walk, reads back `[0, staged_len)` of its distributed K/V byte planes and reports the first block whose fp16 scale is not finite (`[kv-plane] layer N rank R pos0= staged= K=blk .. V=..`); no kernel needed. Global layers only, first `MEMRA_KV_PLANE_SCAN_ROUNDS` verify rounds of the process (one round is one `pos0`; raise it for a fault that appears after the first requests). Syncs per layer, so never a serving or measured arm. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_kv_plane_scan) |
| `MEMRA_KV_REBASE_TRACE` | unset (off) | Strict `1`: prints one `[kv-rebase] new_base= keep_rows= len= retain_from= called from <caller>` line per SWA-ring rebase, with `#[track_caller]` naming the writer, so every site that moves a ring's `base` is listed from one run. One `eprintln` per rebase, not per token, but noise in a measured run. Unset or `0` is off. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_kv_rebase_trace) |
| `MEMRA_KVPROBE_AT=<n[,n…]>` | unset (no cost) | Digests the first `n` rows of a live cache, with `n <= cache.pos` allowed (`worker.rs kvprobe_ats()`), so the KV bytes a longer cold prime writes for rows `[0..n)` compare against the bytes a shorter prime published and restored. Comma-separated list of row counts. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_kvprobe_at) |
| `MEMRA_KVPROBE_LAYERS=1` | unset | With `MEMRA_KVPROBE_AT` set, `1` also emits per-layer digests instead of the whole-state digest alone, to localize a divergence to its layers (`worker.rs kvprobe_layers()`). |  |
| `MEMRA_LAYER_PROBE=1` | unset (off) | Syncs and prints after every forward stage, about one line per layer, to bisect an in-graph `ILLEGAL_ADDRESS` to its (layer, stage). Any set value arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_LOAD_TRACE=1` | unset (silent) | Hybrid loader only: one stderr line per trunk layer with the wall each phase took (`[load-trace] blk.N loaded +Δs (t=Ts)`, plus `tp-armed` after the glm5 TP-2 shard and expert split), so a slow boot is attributed from its own log where ptrace is blocked. Changes nothing. Only `1` arms it. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_load_trace) |
| `MEMRA_MIMO_SPLIT_STAGE_TIMING=1` | unset (off) | Read in `cu/mimo_mixed_attn.cu` by the MiMo mixed-KV global attention dispatch, only on calls at `seq == kMaxSeq` (1048576). Exactly `1` records CUDA events around the tile, two reduce and normalize launches, synchronizes, and prints one `mimo_split_stage_ms` line per call with the program name and four stage times. Kernels and output are unchanged; the sync serializes the stream, so it is a breakdown, not a throughput number. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_mimo_split_stage_timing) |
| `MEMRA_MMQ_SK_DEBUG=1` | unset | `1` prints a per-call `[mmq-sk]` dispatch line from the q4_0 MMQ launcher (in_f, out_f, m, nsm, env-vs-device source, SK or TILE pick). CUDA-side `getenv` (`cu/mmq_q4_0.cu`). |  |
| `MEMRA_MOE_DETERM` | unset (off) | Strict `1`: on layers `il < 4` with `t >= 16`, calls `run_tensor_parallel_routes_nvfp4_prime_grouped` twice on identical inputs and diffs the full output, printing `[moe-determ] il= t= maxdiff= differing=N/M -> IDENTICAL\|NONDETERMINISTIC`. Costs an extra grouped-MoE call per probed layer, so never on in a gated or measured run. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_determ) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_DETERM_BANK` | unset (off) | Strict `1`, sub-door of `MEMRA_MOE_DETERM_STAGE`: also checksums the resident gate weight bank (about 424 MB per rank per layer), the GEMM operand the stage checksums skip. The copy is large (a 45-layer prime moves about 38 GB) and can exceed a request timeout: use it once to establish weight stability, then turn it off. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_determ_bank) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_DETERM_STAGE` | unset (off) | Strict `1`, with `t >= 16`, meaningful beside `MEMRA_MOE_DETERM=1`: per-stage checksums inside the grouped prime (`z_r`/`zs`/`z16` before the gate GEMM, `gate`/`up`/`silu` after it, `a16`/`down_partial` at the tail), plus a `[determ-closure]` line that checksums every buffer the gate kernel reads (CSR, active-expert ids, group offsets, macro scales, pointer table) and the launch geometry. The gate line reports divergence shape (maxdiff, differing count, count above 1e-3). Adds several device-to-host copies per probed layer. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_determ_stage) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_GATE=1` | unset (off) | Byte-identity oracle: each grouped MoE FFN output is compared with the sequential path (the pair contract); a mismatch is a correctness failure, apart from the q8-quantize diff class documented at the gate site. Forces the MoE observation path. Any set value arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_MOE_INPUT_TRACE_DIR=<dir>` | unset | Appends per-layer MoE FFN input rows (t x n_embd f32). Forces the stats-visible dispatch path plus a dtoh per layer; diagnosis only (`hybrid_forward.rs`, `glm5_decode_graph.rs`; checker `tools/validate_moe_input_trace.py`). |  |
| `MEMRA_MOE_OVERLAP` | unset (off) | `1` reports, per spec verify walk, how many of the `t*n_used` expert slots are distinct (unique experts over pairs): the ceiling a union dedup of the t-row sweep could remove. One device-to-host copy of the selection per layer while on; diagnostic only. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_overlap) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_SEL_DUMP=<path>` | unset (off); read once per process | Binary routed-expert selection dump: one record per (routed token, MoE layer) in prime (grouped sigmoid prefill) and decode (the pinned `[sel, w]` readback, and the `vrows_dev` arm at one extra DtoH pair per layer call). Format `memra-moe-sel-v1`, little-endian, no header: `u8 layer, u8 n_sel, n_sel x (u16 expert_id, f32 routing_weight)` in router slot order. Unlike the text taps it is in no dispatch conjunct. Walks whose selection never reaches the host (step TP, W4A16 device-routed EP, `moe_ffn_dev`) refuse an armed dump by name. Buffered 1 MiB, flushed once a second at most. Engagement: `[moe-sel-dump] armed path=...` and `moe_sel_dump::RECORDS`. Reader: `tools/moe_coact.py`. | [LANE.md](../research/moe-coactivation-20260902/LANE.md), [moe_coact.py](../tools/moe_coact.py), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_sel_dump) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_SLRU_PAIRS_TRACE=1` | unset (silent) | One stderr line per MoE layer call wider than 16 rows on the SLRU placement (`[moe-slru-pairs] il= t= n_active= arm= mode= runs= hits= misses= hold_us=`): how the pairs arm got its expert pointers (full-layer row, one pinned admission, or chunked runs), the hits and misses it caused and how long it held the MoE cache lock. Also covers every gemma4_moe MoE call on SLRU, prefill and decode (`[moe-slru-gemma] ...`). Changes nothing. Without it, one `engaged mode=...` line per mode per process is the engagement receipt. Only `1` arms it. | [slru-prefill-program-20261111](../research/slru-prefill-program-20261111/), [gemma-moe-placement-20261111](../research/gemma-moe-placement-20261111/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_slru_pairs_trace) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_STATS=1` | unset (off) | Per-layer expert-cache hit, miss and staged-bytes prints. Forces the stats-visible MoE observation path: pair-batched prefill (`MEMRA_MOE_PAIRS`) stands down and `MEMRA_GLM5_DECODE_GRAPH` refuses while it is set. Any set value arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_MOE_TRACE=<path>` | unset (off) | Appends (layer, step, expert ids) per decode step: routing-locality analysis (`research/scripts/moe_trace_analyze.py`) and the co-occurrence input of the expert-placement mint (`tools/build_expert_placement_map.py --trace`). Diverts MoE dispatch to the host-visible route. The glm5 EP walk is wired in; the device-routed step TP walk refuses an armed tap by name. | [moe_trace_analyze.py](../research/scripts/moe_trace_analyze.py), [build_expert_placement_map.py](../tools/build_expert_placement_map.py), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_trace) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_VROWS_DEDUP_STAT` | off | Measurement instrument, never a serving door: on the vrows host table-build arm, `1` counts expert visits (`t*n_used`) and distinct experts per layer call into `MOE_VROWS_PAIR_VISITS` / `MOE_VROWS_PAIR_DISTINCT`, pricing cross-row expert-slab dedup as `1 - distinct/visits`. Requires `MEMRA_MOE_VROWS_DEV_TABLES=0`, since the device tables remove the host selection it counts. Adds a `HashSet` per layer call, so report it from a dedicated instrument boot, never a timed arm. Not the `moesd` capture, which unions experts over a whole window. | [LANE.md](../research/glm53-flash-bringup-20260827/moe-loc-20260831/LANE.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_vrows_dedup_stat) |
| `MEMRA_MOE_WEIGHT_TRACE=<path>` | unset (off) | Appends every host-routed walk's per-(layer, forward) selected experts and weights, token-major (`<layer> <t> <expert:weight,...>`). Feeds the routing-divergence oracle (`concat_prime_probe`) and the hotness signal of the expert-placement mint (`tools/build_expert_placement_map.py --weight-trace`; maps land via `MEMRA_GLM5_EP_MAP`, co-occurrence comes from `MEMRA_MOE_TRACE`). Rides the existing host route readbacks, no new syncs. Forces the stats-visible dispatch path; the device-routed step TP walk refuses an armed tap by name rather than drop rows. Diagnosis only. | [concat-prime-exact-20260802](../research/concat-prime-exact-20260802/), [build_expert_placement_map.py](../tools/build_expert_placement_map.py), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_moe_weight_trace) |
| `MEMRA_PP_LOGITS` | unset | `<file>`: under `MEMRA_PP_ONLY`, dump the timed pass's last-row logits as raw LE f32 (`prime_cache` in the safetensors arm and under `MEMRA_PP_PRIME=1`, `forward_last` otherwise). The cross-arm drift vector (max_abs, rms_rel, top-k order) for a prefill-GEMM arm. |  |
| `MEMRA_PP_NLL` | unset (any value but `0` arms) | Under `MEMRA_PP_ONLY`, safetensors arm only: teacher-forced prefill exactness on the prime path, argmax disagreement plus mean NLL with the prompt as the tape (position `i` scores prompt token `i+1`), so neither arm can win by reproducing its own output. Use it, not `MEMRA_PREFILL_LOGITS`, for a prefill-GEMM arm: the verify-prefill path has no GEMM/MMQ arm by design and reports `hook entries=0`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_pp_nll) |
| `MEMRA_PREFILL_LOGITS` | unset | `<file>`: dump the verify-class (`decode_step_t`) batched-prefill logit row as raw LE f32. Cross-arm instrument for a kernel that changes the verify class; blind to a prefill-GEMM arm (use `MEMRA_PP_NLL` or `MEMRA_PP_LOGITS`). |  |
| `MEMRA_PREFILL_WIDEN_PROBE` | unset (`1` arms) | `1`: one stderr `[widen-probe]` line per interactive prefill tick naming every input of the solo-widening decision (`configured`, `explicit`, `sole_unfinished`, `fresh` and its four session fields, `queued -> budget`). Reads and prints only. Polarity trap: `ckpt=`/`snapshot=` print `is_none()`, so `ckpt=false` means a checkpoint is armed. The line carries `rule=ckpt-bounds-take-v2`, a build fingerprint that `strings memra-server` finds only in a binary with the freshness fix. Read per tick; off by design, as it is log noise on a busy server. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prefill_widen_probe) |
| `MEMRA_PREFIX_SPLIT_TRACE` | off | `1` D2H-hashes every logical K/V byte plus position and length mirrors at every prefix-entry capture (cold split, spec boundary, fanout and publish snapshots). Exactness-gate diagnostic; it synchronizes and is expensive. |  |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_ANATOMY=1` | unset | `1` syncs the stream around each `prime_layers`/`forward_last` stage and prints cumulative ms per stage (attn_full, gdn_linear, moe, dense, norms). Attribution shares only: the syncs serialize overlap (`hybrid_forward.rs`, `qwen_prime_graph.rs`). | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prime_anatomy) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_PROF` | OFF | `1` prints sync-bounded per-phase wall inside the prime layer walk as `[prime-prof]`, once per prime call (norm+qkv, attention, o_proj, MoE; the hyper trunk splits glue, mixer and FFN). Each mark synchronizes the stream, so absolute time inflates; read the split. It serializes the walk. Diagnostic only. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prime_prof) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_ROW_RECEIPT` | off (unset or `0`) | `1` prints the per-prime-chunk `[prime-row] kind=.. rows=.. a4_launches=.. a4_projections=.. logits_sha=.. top1=.. margin=..` receipt, the cold-versus-restored prime row comparison the A4 activation lane reads. Off prints nothing and skips the hash and margin scan of the last-position logits. On reproduces the former always-on lines byte for byte. An explanatory diagnostic kept while its subject lane is open, so no decide-by. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_prime_row_receipt) |
| `MEMRA_PRIME_TRACE=<path>` | unset | Chunk-invariance bisect seam: appends one JSONL row per (chunk, layer) with a hash of that layer's last-row post-residual hidden. Forces a dtoh and a host hash per layer, so never on in a measured or gated run (`hybrid_forward.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_PROMPT_TOKENS` | unset | Diagnostic-only explicit token-id prompt for `verify_shape_bisect`: ids separated by any non-digit characters, so a failing serving tape replays without tokenizer or template ambiguity. Wins over `MEMRA_PROMPT`. |  |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_ROUTE_SYNC` | unset (off) | `1`, with the device MoE router armed, restores the host arm's per-layer stream synchronize in the graph driver (`moe_route_slots`), to separate route-kernel cost from missing-sync cost in a regression.
