Diagnostic only, never a serving arm. | [devtwin](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/devtwin/), [PROFILE-9.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-9.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_route_sync) |
| `MEMRA_Q8_CENSUS=1` | unset (off) | Attributes every `quantize_q8_1` launch (`quantize_q8_1`, `_view`, the slot-fed `_into`) to its Rust call site (`#[track_caller]`) and prints a cumulative `[q8-census]` histogram (count, share, file:line, m, in_f) every 256 calls; `=<n>` prints every n, `0` or empty is off. The threshold is low because under the decode graph door call sites run once per capture, so the census reads sites and their multiplicity per capture. Never set while serving; off costs one OnceLock read per call. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q8_census) |
| `MEMRA_RAW_COPY_TRACE` | off | `1` prints operands and a backtrace when `tp::raw_copy_bytes` fails, since a raw D2D copy carries no call site otherwise. The returned error always carries bytes, dst and src. |  |
| `MEMRA_RESIDENCY_CENSUS=1` | unset | run-gen: any value but `0` prints the model residency census report after load, the evidence that separates an arm that ran flat from one that never engaged on the checkpoint (`run_gen.rs`, `model.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_ROPE_ROWS_LAYER` | unset (every layer) | Debug bisection: engage the full t-row pass only on layer `N` or the inclusive range `A-B`; other layers take the per-row flow. |  |
| `MEMRA_ROUND_GRAPH_CHECK=1` | unset (off) | Round-graph bisect: runs the captured gemma round body eagerly with no capture or replay, splitting body-semantics bugs from replay-mechanics bugs. With `MEMRA_DEBUG_SPEC=1` it also prints the round's state. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_ROWS_TAB_STALE_SCAN` | unset (off) | Strict `1`: keeps a host shadow of the last table staged under each retired memo key and prints `[rows-tab] STALE #n lookup #m rank= t= key= moved=v[row0],len[row0]` when a key repeats with different contents, naming the words that moved; the lookup counter proves the path ran. Costs a host hash lookup and a small clone per rank per layer per verify round, so never on in serving or a measured run. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_rows_tab_stale_scan) |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_DISPATCH_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | Prints a `[sigrouter-dev]` line for every engagement of Step-3.7's resident zero-DtoH sigmoid dispatch arm: layer, token count, experts used, clamp form and gate/up interleave state. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_LOGIT_TRACE=<path>` | unset (off) | Writes the first real decode router-logit row per layer, bit-preserving (raw f32 bits), with correction bias, original-id active mask, scaling and normalization metadata; `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_REPLAY` replays it. | `MEMRA_SKEY_PROBE=1` | unset (off) | Sampled-draft-graph key probe: prints the burst-entry sampler facts (`[skey] burst sampled/pure_temp/top_k/top_p ... graph_s_parked`). No numeric effect. Only `1` arms it; read once per process. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_skey_probe) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_ANATOMY` | unset (off) | `1` arms eager MTP draft-step phase timers (glue, attn, ffn, head) with a cumulative stderr summary every 128 steps; only `1` arms it, read once per process. Phase boundaries sync the stream, so absolute time inflates and the breakdown is the signal. Diagnostic only, never a serving setting. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_anatomy) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_BOUNDARY_TRACE` | off | `1` prints every sampled spec boundary draw on stderr, `[spec-boundary] site=<cold-prime\|burst-tail-commit\|burst-tail-nopending\|flush-pending\|restore-suffix-feed\|restore-full-cover> sampled=<id> argmax=<id> deviates=<0\|1>`, and every sampled burst that enters on a carried pending token, `[spec-carry] site=burst-entry token=<id> event=<n>`. `burst-tail-commit` fires only for a penalised request under `MEMRA_SPEC_PEN_SESSION=0`; under `MEMRA_SPEC_SAMPLED_BOUNDARY=0` the tail takes an argmax and prints nothing. No behavior change; one extra device argmax per boundary. The hit gate asserts on it. | [SAMPLED-PENDING-CARRY.md](decisions/SAMPLED-PENDING-CARRY.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_boundary_trace) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2_DEBUG` | off | `1` prints the first failing fa2 precheck clause once per clause id (`[spec-fa2] precheck FAIL clause=...`), so a vacuous fa2 door can be told apart from a slow one. No behavior change. |  |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_FA2_LAYER` | unset (no code reads it) | Debug bisection meant to engage the fa2 join on one layer index only, every other layer running the per-column program. No code reads this name. |  |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_NAN_SCAN` | unset (off) | A level, not a boolean. `1` scans each trunk layer's residual and traps on the first non-finite row, printing the bad-row bitmap (`rows[t] map=XXXX counts=[...]`) and a `[verify-arm] layer N arm=fused\|join\|percol` line naming the attention arm taken. `2` adds intra-layer scans that split attention output from routed-MoE output. Every scan point syncs, which also bounds where an asynchronous CUDA fault was launched. The level changes which fault manifests: quote it with every localization and never compare counts across levels. Unset, `0` or any other value is off; every call site sits behind `spec_nan_scan()`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_nan_scan) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE=1` | unset (off) | Per-round wall decomposition of the spec loop (draft, verify, accept plus commit), printed once at loop end through spec-stats. No tracing and no extra syncs: each phase is naturally sync-bounded. `MEMRA_SPEC_PP_ANATOMY=1` arms it too; `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE_SYNC=1` adds the sync that reads the verify split correctly. | [verify-economics-20260802](../research/verify-economics-20260802/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_phase) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE_SYNC=1` | unset (off) | Syncs the stream right after the verify walk is issued so `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE`'s split reads correctly. Without it `verify-issue` is host queueing while the GPU already runs and `verify-wait` is only the residual drain, one overlapped interval. With it, `verify-issue` absorbs the GPU wall and `verify-wait` collapses to the readback; the round wall must not move, and if it does, the queueing was exposed. Only `1` arms it; diagnostic only. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_phase_sync) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PP_ANATOMY=1` | unset | `1` prints one-shot PP-2 spec-verify boundary pricing to the server log (reverse hop, stage 0, TX, stage 1 plus head) without nsys. Engages only at exactly two stages; the report never implies it measured middle stages (`spec.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_PROF=1` | unset (off); read once per process | One `[spec-prof]` line per glm5 spec request after the first burst, with each phase from admission to the first streamed token in ms: `since_admit`, `session` (`cache_alloc`, `prime`, `capture`, `anchor`, `draft_alloc`), round 1 (`draft_prime`, `draft`, `verify`, `accept`, `roll`, `maint`), `burst1` with `hook_ms` (subtract it for the engine-only wall), `first_emit_ms`, `step_ms`, plus drafter-prime depth fields (`arm=`, `sink_alloc`, `tap_dtoh`, `draft_kv_mb`, `free_mb_before/after`). Also `[spec-prof-rounds]` per burst and one `[spec-prof-summary]` per session. Phase boundaries sync the stream: attribute with it, claim with untraced TTFT. Not `MEMRA_SPEC_TRACE` or `MEMRA_TTFT_TRACE`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_prof) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_ROUND_PROF` | off | `1` prints the whole spec round's wall time every 32 rounds, to reconcile a round against its measured parts (`MEMRA_SPEC_ANATOMY`, `MEMRA_TCOL_PROF`). Diagnostic only; adds no syncs beyond the host readbacks the round already does. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_round_prof) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_SETUP_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | Per-call wall decomposition of the serve spec burst's setup and tail segments, pinning the per-burst fixed cost; the round loop's internals are `MEMRA_SPEC_PHASE`'s job. Only `1` arms it. `tools/spec-burst-boundary-gate.py` sets it. | [spec-serving-20260801](../research/spec-serving-20260801/) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_STATS=1` | unset (off) | Per-slot accept histogram and draft-length histogram, plus the per-burst `[draft-mask]` mask and clone line on constrained MTP bursts; the DFlash and gemma spec loops print their acceptance under it too. |  |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_TRACE` | off | General spec-phase instrument (`spec_phase.rs`); emit tags belong to the calling family, so glm5 keeps `[glm5-phase]`. `1`: one line per burst with rounds, per-phase totals and per-round means for draft, verify, accept, rollback and source maintenance. `2`: adds the verify sub-split `[glm5-phase-v]` (`vkda` with its `scan` share, `vmla`, `vrest`). Every phase boundary synchronizes the stream, so the numbers are shares for attribution, never round walls or perf rows. Other values read as off. Alias `MEMRA_GLM5_SPEC_TRACE`; when both are set to different levels the general name wins with one `[spec-trace]` line. | [loop-port-20260830](../research/glm53-flash-bringup-20260827/loop-port-20260830/), [verify-batch-20260830](../research/glm53-flash-bringup-20260827/verify-batch-20260830/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_trace) |
| `MEMRA_SPILL_STATS=1` | unset (off) | When server requests finish, prints cumulative positioned-read snapshots (reads, logical bytes, errors, short reads, `config_fallbacks`, runtime mmap `fallbacks`, buffer waits, worker ring-full events) and stage counters (worker positioned-read, owner blocking-read and wait ms, H2D submissions, direct-window over-read bytes). `run-gen` prints the same fields per decode window as `spill stages ...`. Snapshots are totals, not per-request deltas; do not sum them. Only `1` arms it. | [spill-f-20260919](../research/spill-f-20260919/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spill_stats) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_TP_TIMING` | off | `1` prints cumulative wall-clock counters for host-visible MoE routing, W4A16 whole-expert EP, the shared expert, the NVFP4 device-routes program and rank-local TP attention decode, plus an 8-phase split of the v1 attention driver (pos, qkv, normrope, gate, append, attn, oproj, shadow). Each phase lap drains every stream, so the split perturbs absolute wall time; never on in a gated or receipt run. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_tp_timing) |
| `MEMRA_TICK_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | memra-server: per-tick phase timing of the batched scheduler to stderr; the prime walker and the glm5 prime rendezvous (`[glm5-prime-rendezvous]`) print their chunk steps under it too, the spec boundary publication its `[specpub]` line, and the prefix cache its `[prefix-loan]` reconcile lines (charged bytes against the bytes its entries and adopted planes hold, memra#1221 part 2). Diagnosis only. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_TTFT_TRACE=1` | unset (off) | memra-server: one `[ttft]` line per completion request with request-parse, HTTP admission, worker queue wait, tokenization, pre-prime setup, prime, first-decode wait, first serialized SSE body byte, cumulative boundary and total timings. Disabled requests allocate no trace state. Only `1` arms it. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_ttft_trace) |
| `MEMRA_VISION_DEBUG=<dir>` | unset | Vision-tower stage dumps (`rust_<tag>.bin`, f32 LE) for the HF parity bisect, from the qwen (`vision.rs`), gemma (`vision_gemma.rs`), GLM-5 (`vision_glm5.rs`) and Step (`vision_step.rs`) towers. `gemma-vision-oracle` and `step-vision-oracle` set it to their own out dir. |  |
| `MEMRA_W8_TRACE` | off | `1` prints one `[w8-mirror] built in_f=.. out_f=.. mirrors=N` line per q8_0 mirror the W8 hybrid half builds. It shows whether the half fired at all: the head and shared expert can reach the device through the rows fast path or the fused dual-silu launcher instead of `matvec_bf16_into`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_w8_trace) |

## Gate, bench and CI inputs

Inputs that only gate binaries, test fixtures, CI and release scripts read: red arms, fault injection, gate selectors, fixture paths and harness knobs. None belongs in a serving launch; `MEMRA_GPU_LEASE_FILE` (set by `tools/memra-gpu-run`) ties a release-evidence run to its leased cards. The gates that use these rows are in [TESTING.md](TESTING.md), and release evidence in [RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md](RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md).

| Flag | Default | What it does | Record |
|---|---|---|---|
| (no flag) A4 operand-statistics arming: `a4_stats_enable()` / `a4_stats_take()` (process API) | unarmed; only `qwen-a4-refit-stats` arms it | Engine API, not an env door. While armed, `matmul_prefill` accumulates each stamped projection's operand absolute max (amax) and a 64-bin log2 histogram on device, then diverts to the ordinary W4A8 GEMM, so the whole trunk runs the default arithmetic and the statistics describe a normal prime, not A4-perturbed activations. `a4_stats_take()` reads the counters back and disarms. No serving path arms it. | [DESIGN.md](../research/qwen-fp4-activation-mint-20260909/DESIGN.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#row-573) |
| `--spec-trace <tokens>` (gate binary, diagnostic) | off | Decay-diagnosis instrument: per-round records (accept position, draft and target fork margins, target entropy, carrier drift against the verify chunk's true wide rows) over every `--prompts` prompt, with a plain byte-identity twin per prompt that fails on divergence. Only adds reads and swaps the device accept-argmax for the bit-identical host argmax. Analysis: `spec/mtp10/analyze-trace.py`. Never a serving mode. | [mtp10](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/mtp10/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#row-1514) |
| `arm_attention_tp_join_refusal_for_gate()` / `attention_tp_last_join_for_gate()` (process API) | unarmed, no readback (diagnostic gate APIs) | The first injects refusal word 40043/40044 once after the selected attention join, into the sticky plane MoE uses; a precommit refusal restores both cache planes and poisons retry. The second reads the preserved final-layer partials and the dedicated joined outputs, never a reused MoE buffer. Only the attention gate uses them, including its six rank/position refusal cells. No runtime or serving selector. | [attention-tp-runtime-20260907.md](../research/dsv4f-2card-1m-20260904/attention-tp-runtime-20260907.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#arm_attention_tp_join_refusal_for_gate) |
| `MEMRA_GSG_*` | see `gemma_sample_gate`'s header | Gate-only knobs for `memra-engine/src/bin/gemma_sample_gate.rs`, read by nothing the server runs: `MEMRA_GSG_SEEDS` (seeds per arm, 600), `MEMRA_GSG_TOKENS` (positions compared, 4), `MEMRA_GSG_TEMP` / `MEMRA_GSG_TOPK` / `MEMRA_GSG_TOPP` (the sampled row, default the vendor row 1.0 / 64 / 0.95), `MEMRA_GSG_K` (draft depth, 5), `MEMRA_GSG_PROMPT` (comma-separated ids; must clear `PRIME_MIN_T`) and `MEMRA_GSG_RED` (`boundary` = the red arm: greedy prime under a sampled burst, which the chi-square arm must fail). They change what the gate measures, never what the server serves. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_gsg_seeds) |
| `MEMRA_SELSHAPE_*` (`sel_shape_probe` instrument binary only: `_ARMS`, `_REPS`, `_T`, `_SELECTED`, `_EXPERTS`, `_FF`, `_HIDDEN`) | unset: `_REPS` 25, `_T` 6, geometry 512 experts, 10 selected, hidden 2560, ff 640 | Shape-selection instrument for the `selgroup` ladder: `_ARMS` takes comma-separated selgroup specs, and the probe runs the shipped sel kernels and candidate `(g, rows)` reshapes interleaved with the bit-identical `g=32,rows=4` control, reporting control-relative ratios only, never serving numbers. `_REPS` sets the interleaved passes; the geometry overrides default to the qwen4_exp census shapes. Read only in `src/bin/sel_shape_probe.rs`. | [downsel](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/spec/downsel/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#row-1494) |
| `qwen4exp_real_gate --host-probe` (CLI, no env) | off | Host-side census from /proc and `sched_getcpu`: voluntary context switches count blocking syncs (a spinning CUDA wait shows zero), nonvoluntary switches count preemption, and per-step `sched_getcpu` counts launch-thread migrations; the /proc parse runs only at segment boundaries. Emits `# host-thread` rows and the `vol_cs`, `nonvol_cs` and `launch_cpu_migrations` fields of `# ladder-ab-verdict`. Independently, `# ladder-jitter` (sd, cv, p99, max, count above 1.5x median) rides every ladder run. | [PROFILE-12.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-12.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#row-1503) |
| `qwen4exp_real_gate --ladder-ab-seam <seam> [--ladder-ab-rounds n] [--ladder-ab-steps n]` (CLI, no env) | off (when armed: 3 rounds, 16 steps) | Within-prefill interleaved decode A/B over one `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS` name: after each rung's timing row the arms alternate off/on on the same state, lead flipped on odd reps, so both share one prefill. Escalates to x3 then x5 when either arm's spread exceeds 0.5% or the verdict sits inside 2x the pooled spread. Only for seams whose state rebuilds from the token history (`plecache` qualifies); a seam that latches a layout or mutates a device mirror needs per-arm processes. Seam names are validated before the checkpoint loads; restore is exact (`# ladder-ab-restore`). Emits `# ladder-ab`, `# ladder-ab-verdict`, `# ab-profile`. | [PROFILE-12.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-12.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_seams) |
| `set_c4_host_copy_elision_for_gate()` (process API) | OFF (gate-only) | Active-C4 profile seam for gates, not a request or serving flag. After a canonical prime, snapshot or restore, a complete recent-row sidecar may serve newly emitted rows while host publication is skipped; snapshots refuse while it is on. No default and no graph admission. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_c4_host_copy_elision_for_gate) |
| `set_prefill_grouped_all` (qwen4exp_gpu library seam, no env of its own; set by `--tp2-prefill-gate` around its prime forward only) | off (`PREFILL_GROUPED_ALL` init false) | Gate instrument: runs a `HeadMode::All` single-card forward on the grouped MoE executor instead of the per-expert one, so the TP2 class gate's prime regime compares grouped against grouped and isolates the TP2 expert-half split from the executor difference (which dominates by about 100x). Set by `--tp2-prefill-gate` around its prime forward only and restored at once. Off is today's behavior and the reference program the goldens capture; nothing in serving reads it. | [TP2-CLASS-GATE.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/round2-box-receipts/TP2-CLASS-GATE.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_prefill_grouped_all) |
| `set_tp_ep_ar_refusal_words_for_gate()` (process API) | no injection (red-arm diagnostic) | Exclusive walk-lock control for the sticky reduction error words. The gate injects rank-0 40043 and rank-1 40044, then requires no cache or position commit and no retry enqueue. `[0, 0]` clears the words after draining, never the failed-request state. Gate: `dsv4_tp_ep_gate`. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#set_tp_ep_ar_refusal_words_for_gate) |
| `MEMRA_A4_DISABLE=1` | unset (artifact-driven dispatch) | `qwen-nll-kl` harness only, read by no serving binary. `PrefillFp4::from_gguf` refuses the activation program before the weights are stamped, so a calibrated artifact scores through the plain W4A8 path; against the W4A8 arm the result must be bitwise, KL exactly 0. Clearing the config after load would not work, because `matmul_prefill` reads the stamp. Any set value arms it, `0` included. | [DESIGN.md](../research/qwen-fp4-activation-mint-20260909/DESIGN.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_a4_disable) |
| `MEMRA_A4_PRIME_VS_DECODE=1` | unset (off) | `qwen-nll-kl` harness only, never a serving flag. Per scored window, prints how many of the first 64 teacher-forced tokens a free greedy continuation from the identical prefix reproduces on the same artifact: a prime-path versus decode-path agreement check that needs no second artifact. Adds one greedy generation per window. Any set value arms it. | [DESIGN.md](../research/qwen-fp4-activation-mint-20260909/DESIGN.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_a4_prime_vs_decode) |
| `MEMRA_ADMIT_PREDICT_BOOT_SUBPROC_CASE` | unset | Internal test-harness discriminator for `worker::tests::admit_predict_enforce_boot_subprocess_runner`. Spawned test subprocesses set it (`single_gpu`, `pp`, `explicit_tp_ep`, `auto_ep`, `env_unset_after_load`) to drive isolated boot paths that test enforcement's fail-closed boot on single-GPU and multi-device topologies. Serving never sets it. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_admit_predict_boot_subproc_case) |
| `MEMRA_ARTIFACT_LOCK=<file>` / `MEMRA_REWRITE_RECEIPT=<file>` | unset | Rewrite gate binaries (decode-batch-gate, graph-decode-gate, prime-batch-gate, run-spec): `MEMRA_REWRITE_RECEIPT` selects the receipt output path and `MEMRA_ARTIFACT_LOCK` binds the receipt to the exact artifact lock; a receipt without the lock refuses. Gate-harness inputs, not serving knobs. |  |

Skipped when the 9B NVFP4 model is absent. | [request-fault-20260922](../research/request-fault-20260922/), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_ci_faultgate) |

Both refuse the load unless the process called `arm_ar_phase_door_for_gate()` before `Dsv4Gpu::load` (only `dsv4-ar-phase-gate` does) and refuse outside the all-layer TP/EP topology; other values refuse. Read once at load. The gate's `--red-delay`, `--disarmed` and `--report` modes prove it non-vacuous. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_dsv4_ar_phase) |
| `MEMRA_DSV4_GATE_REPEATS` | `3` (measurement binary only) | Repeat count for `dsv4_decode_rate_gate`'s paired ABBA modes: `1` is a two-row-per-arm smoke, `3` yields six rows per arm; zero or non-numeric values fall back to 3. `dsv4_plain_perf_gate` fixes three repeats in code. Selects no serving program. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_dsv4_gate_repeats) |

| `MEMRA_E4B_GRAPH_GATE=N` (gemma-gate) | unset (cell skipped); N defaults to 64 when unparsable | `gemma-gate` E4B graph-door stream gate: runs `generate()` with the door off, then on, on fresh caches; the streams must be identical (the warmup-side-effect and exec-update oracle). |  |

| `MEMRA_FAULT_INJECT_CACHE_SALT=<salt>` | unset (off) | Fault-injection door: a request whose `cache_salt` equals the value panics inside its own guarded `fault-inject` step at the top of the tick. It must fail typed (`code: worker_fault`, 500 or the SSE error event), its peers must finish byte-identical, and `worker_respawns_total` must not move; `tools/request-fault-gate.py` asserts that. | `MEMRA_FP8_MMQ_NLL=<txt>` | unset | fp8-mmq-stream: mean token NLL over a real text window in one prefill, the quality-sanity cell (`fp8_mmq_stream.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_FP8_MMQ_TF=<log>` | unset | fp8-mmq-stream: teacher-forces from a prior run's `stream ids: [...]` line, so arms compare on an identical trajectory (`fp8_mmq_stream.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_GATE_CANARY=1` | unset (off) | `decode-batch-gate` test-only plumbing canary: feeds gate1's batched lane one wrong token at step 1, so every draw diverges early and the fraction rule must fail. | `MEMRA_GATE_DUMP_TOKENS=1` | unset (off) | Test-only: `gemma-gate` prints the plain and spec token streams, for exact on/off benchmark harnesses. Does not alter inference. Only `1` arms it. |  |
| `MEMRA_GATE_KEY` | set by the gate | `serve-gemma4-tools-gate.sh`: the API-key variable its codex client reads (`env_key`); the gate sets it to its own key for each client run. Shell-side. |  |
| `MEMRA_GATE_LEND_ABOVE=1` | unset (off) | `session-gate` test-only: before each checkpoint rewind, lends every trunk layer's rows `[0, committed)`, which reach past the checkpoint (as a prefix entry whose boundary capture sat above it reads them), holds the lends through the turn, rewinds into a fresh cache as the server does, and fails if the lent bytes change or no rewind ran under a lend. Only `1` arms it. | [SPEC-BOUNDARY-LEND](decisions/SPEC-BOUNDARY-LEND.md) |
| `MEMRA_GATE_LOGDIR` | `$TMPDIR/memra-argmax-margin-gate`; fast-gate: a new dated directory per run | Raw log directory for `argmax-margin-gate.sh` and `tools/fast-gate/fast-gate.sh`. Shell-side. |  |
| `MEMRA_GATE_MTP_DRAFT` | unset (no external drafter; the attach assertion is skipped) | `<draft.gguf>` hands the spec gate tools (`spec-on-cache-hit-gate.sh`, `spec-cache-gate.sh`, `spec-cache-mixed-gate.sh`, `sampled-restore-ab.sh`) the Qwen drafter, which each forwards to the server as `MEMRA_MTP_DRAFT`, never as `MEMRA_DRAFT`. | `MEMRA_Q4E_IDXSEL_AUDIT` | unset (off) | `1`, with `idxsel` armed, copies the score slab back anyway and recomputes `top_blocks_ascending` on the host from the same bytes, hard-comparing block ids and emitted order; any difference is an error. Receipt line `# idxsel-audit rows=<n> mismatched=<n> deepest_blocks=<n>`: `rows=0` means it never engaged, and `deepest_blocks` shows whether the audit reached depth. | [PROFILE-11.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-11.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_idxsel_audit) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_MEASURE_LOCK` (equivalently `qwen4exp_real_gate --measure-lock <path>`) | unset (no serialisation) | Path to an advisory lock file that `qwen4exp_real_gate` takes in two modes: `LOCK_EX` around timed blocks (ladder rung timing rounds and each interleaved `--ladder-ab-seam` pair, released between reps) and `LOCK_SH` around each prefill chunk, released at every chunk boundary so a waiting exclusive gets in within one chunk. Untimed cells should take `flock -s` on the same path and yield the same way. Each timing prints `# measure-lock what=<block> mode=<ex\|sh> path=<lock> waited_s=<n>`. A lock that cannot be opened or taken is a hard error. Unset changes no arithmetic or timing. | [PROFILE-12.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-12.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_measure_lock) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_MEASURE_LOCK_PREFILL` | `sh` (prefill chunks take the shared lock) | `off` skips the per-prefill-chunk `LOCK_SH` while every timed block stays exclusive; any other value keeps `sh`. Use `off` only while a coarser sibling protocol holds `LOCK_EX` across whole cells (which starves a per-chunk shared acquirer), say so in the cell's receipt, and prefer `sh`. | [PROFILE-12.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/perf/PROFILE-12.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_measure_lock_prefill) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_MEASURE_LOCK_TIMEOUT_S` | 7200 (seconds) | Instrument deadline: how long `MeasureLock` retries `flock(..., LOCK_NB)` before it fails. Generous on purpose, since a legitimate wait is a sibling's whole cell; lower it only in a harness that knows its siblings' cell lengths. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_measure_lock_timeout_s) |
| `MEMRA_Q4E_PLECACHE_AUDIT` | unset (off) | `1`, with `plecache` armed, recomputes the full `host_ngram_ids` twin and hard-compares each chunk's ids; any difference is an error. | `MEMRA_Q4E_SEAMS` | unset | Comma list of qwen4_exp seam names (`projstack`, `hcmicro`, `selv3`, `vfuse`, ...; `name=0` disables, `name=value` for valued seams) that `qwen4exp_gpu::apply_env_seams` applies at startup of the qwen4exp gate binaries, so a gate can prove a seam before its library default flips; an unknown name or bad value prints a warning and applies nothing. | `MEMRA_Q4E_TP2_GATE_RED` | unset (`none`) | Red arm for the qwen4_exp TP2 class gate: injects one defect into the TP2 MoE split so the near-tie band can be shown to separate a wrong program. Values: `skip-peer-moe` (drop the peer card's routed contribution), `peer-local-ids` (send peer-owned experts to card 0's bank at their peer-local slot), `reverse-peer-weights` (reverse the peer half's slot weights per token), `none`/`0`/unset. An unknown value refuses at the first MoE layer. A red arm passes by being loud (`max_rel` past `TP2_RED_FLOOR` 1e-3 or a broken greedy tape) and by having engaged the peer card (`peer_slots > 0`). Paired instrument: `--tp2-class-calibrate`. | [ROUND2-STATUS.md](../research/qwen4exp-bringup-20260829/kvq/ROUND2-STATUS.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_q4e_tp2_gate_red) |

| `MEMRA_REPLAY_HDUMP=<dir>` | unset (required in directory mode) | replay-acceptance hidden-state dumps (`<stem>.hiddens` plus `.meta.json`) for bulk distillation extraction; a directory in directory mode, where it is required, a file path in single-file mode. Skip draft chains there with a huge `MEMRA_REPLAY_STRIDE`. |  |
| `MEMRA_REPLAY_NLL=1` | unset | `1` makes replay-acceptance's trunk arm also accumulate the mean teacher-forced NLL over the forced tape (`spec.rs replay_acceptance`). |  |
| `MEMRA_SERVE_VERIFY_TIMEOUT_S=N` | 180 | `memra model verify serve`: readiness deadline in seconds for the temporary native server. A timeout fails the serve gate and never promotes support. |  |
| `MEMRA_SG_SEEDS` / `MEMRA_SG_TOKENS` / `MEMRA_SG_TEMP` / `MEMRA_SG_TOPK` / `MEMRA_SG_TOPP` / `MEMRA_SG_REP` / `MEMRA_SG_FREQ` / `MEMRA_SG_PRESENT` / `MEMRA_SG_LASTN` / `MEMRA_SG_PROMPT` | 2000 / 4 / 0.8 / 0 / 1.0 / 1.0 / 0.0 / 0.0 / 8192 / a fixed 24-id prompt | dspark_sample_gate hist mode, the distribution gate for the dspark route's sampled admission: seeds per arm, positions compared, temperature, top-k, top-p, repetition, frequency and presence penalties, penalty window (the serve API's cross-path bound) and comma-separated prompt token ids. Spec-on vs trunk-only per-position two-sample chi-square; with a penalty set both arms carry it. The tiny mode takes no knobs; t0 also gates the penalties-at-T=0 refusal. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_sg_freq) |
| `MEMRA_SIG_ROUTER_REPLAY=<path>` | unset (cell skipped) | `kernel-check` input for the served-logit host-versus-device sigmoid-router replay cell. An empty, malformed or duplicate-layer file, or any selected-id or routing-weight-bit disagreement, fails the required manifest gate. |  |
| `MEMRA_SKIP_PERF_CI` | unset (`0`) | Retired input kept as a refusal: `MEMRA_SKIP_PERF_CI=1` refuses the push. It cannot skip content-bound qualification. | [RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md](RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_DEMOTE_AT=N` | unset (load-triggered demotion only) | Test door read by memra-server, never set outside an exactness gate. Forces the spec-to-batched demotion handoff when a session reaches N generated tokens, regardless of load, and demotes only that session. Load-triggered demotion (`active >= MEMRA_SPEC_GATE_HIGH`) varies in timing and batch shape, and batched decode is not bit-identical across shapes, so this pins B=1 and leaves the handoff (pending flush, `(cache, next_pred)` transfer, position, stream continuation) as the only variable. Logs `FORCED at DEMOTE_AT=N (test door)`. Used by `research/spec-gate-20260806/exactness.py` and `tools/seeded-invariance-gate.py`. | [exactness.py](../research/spec-gate-20260806/exactness.py), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_spec_demote_at) |
| `MEMRA_SPEC_ONLY=1` | unset | gemma-gate: `1` skips the plain-greedy reference arm in the dflash and MTP spec cells, a timing-only run (`gemma_gate.rs`; `full-board-bench.sh` and `local-ci.sh` set it). |  |
| `MEMRA_ST_NGEN=N` | unset (gate skipped); N=32 when the value does not parse | run-safetensors greedy continuation gate: generates N tokens for the native-vs-GGUF byte compare (`run_safetensors.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_ST_PORT` | 8180 | `serve-st-gate.sh` server port; the port guard checks it before boot. Shell-side. |  |
| `MEMRA_STEP_EP_BENCH_ITERS` / `MEMRA_STEP_EP_BENCH_WARMUP` | iterations unset (bench off); warmup 5 | ep-step-fp8-gate EP microbenchmark: `MEMRA_STEP_EP_BENCH_ITERS` in 1..1000 enables it (unset, empty or `0` keeps it off); `MEMRA_STEP_EP_BENCH_WARMUP` takes 0..1000. Out-of-range or non-integer values refuse. |  |
| `MEMRA_STEP_EP_DYNAMIC_CAPACITY_TOKENS` / `MEMRA_STEP_EP_NUMERIC_ITERS` | initial token count + 2 / 50 | ep-step-fp8-gate: dynamic owner-grouped workspace capacity in tokens, and numeric probe repetitions (must be positive) (`ep_step_fp8_gate.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_STEP_OOM_FAULT=<n>` | unset or `0` (off by design) | Step-OOM fault injection: forges a quoted synthetic CUDA OOM on the next `n` session steps at the spec phase's step dispatch and the plain path's batched decode chunk (one budget; the step is skipped). `batch:<n>` aims it only at plain batched chunks with at least two sessions (`tools/health-fault-gate.sh` arm j). Downstream logic is real: park vs honest error, `oom_teardown`, `park_requeue`, `MEMRA_STEP_OOM_RETRIES`. `1` proves the park branch; `n > MEMRA_STEP_OOM_RETRIES` walks one session into the bounded-retry error. Malformed values warn and stay off. Never set on a serving box. | [BATCH-OOM-RECOVER-DEFAULT.md](decisions/BATCH-OOM-RECOVER-DEFAULT.md), [DAY38.md](../research/spill-b-20260919/DAY38.md), [DAY49.md](../research/spill-b-20260919/DAY49.md), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_step_oom_fault) |
| `MEMRA_STEP_PRO_RECEIPT` | unset | Path to the additional Step PRO receipt, required when `MEMRA_HARDWARE_GATE=step-pro` (the push refuses without it). Never a replacement for the generic full input and build record. | [RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md](RELEASE-QUALIFICATION.md) |
| `MEMRA_TEST_DSV4_DEFAULT_CHILD`, `MEMRA_TEST_REPLAY_DRAIN_FAILURE_CHILD` | unset (the parent test runs) | `cfg(test)`-only child-process markers; non-test builds never read them, and they are not doors. `MEMRA_TEST_DSV4_DEFAULT_CHILD=<cadence>:<dense>` (each `0` or `1`) makes the `dsv4_tp_ep_sampled_perf_gate` test child check the environment initialization of the replay-cadence and dense exact-tail defaults and their thread-local overrides, without CUDA allocation. `MEMRA_TEST_REPLAY_DRAIN_FAILURE_CHILD=error:<rank>` or `drop:<rank>` (rank `0` or `1`) injects a failed completion in the `dsv4_graph` fail-stop test; each child must abort (SIGABRT) before the captured-storage destructor runs. |  |
| `MEMRA_TEST_MIMO_MKL_RT` | unset (the ignored test stays ignored) | Test-only absolute path to a standalone oneMKL 2024.2 `libmkl_rt.so.2` for the ignored MiMo PCM-to-mel source corpus check in `memra-reference`; running that test without it fails. Set `MKL_THREADING_LAYER=SEQUENTIAL` before the test process starts. Does not enable audio serving. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_test_mimo_mkl_rt) |
| `MEMRA_TIER_BATTERY_LOCK_DIR` | unset (the two canonical rig locks) | Test seam read by `tools/tier-battery.py` (`lock_table`) and `tools/tier-rig-bootstrap.sh`. `=<existing dir>` re-roots the two lock names under it (`<dir>/memra-5090.lock`, `<dir>/memra-gpu.lock`) so the battery's CPU suite never contends a real lock. The collector's `--execute`/`--dry-run` and any lock-holding bootstrap refuse under it (exit 2, before any lock) unless `--private-lock-dir-for-tests` is also passed; the flag without the seam refuses too. A seamed process says so on stderr and stamps `"seam": "<dir>"` into every `lock.json` and manifest, which `--validate` refuses from a different seam. Not a third lock name: never set it in a launcher, gate or campaign. | [TESTING.md](TESTING.md#collector-toolstier-batterypy), [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_tier_battery_lock_dir) |
| `MEMRA_TP_DEVICES` / `MEMRA_TP_TOKENS` / `MEMRA_TP_REPS` | `0,1` / 1, 2 or 3 per gate / 5 | Step TP/EP gates: comma-separated gate device ordinals (the allowed rank count depends on the gate), test token count, and `tp-step-resident-device-gate`'s interleave count (host-arm/device-arm alternation per rep). They select fixture topology and gate repetition only. | [history](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#memra_tp_devices) |
| `MEMRA_TP_GATE_GROW` / `MEMRA_TP_GATE_SWA` | unset (off) | tp-step-attention-gate: `1` exercises transaction growth (`MEMRA_TP_GATE_GROW`) or the official sliding-window layer (`MEMRA_TP_GATE_SWA`). Values other than empty, `0` or `1`, and an SWA request on a non-window layer, fail closed. |  |
| `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE=K` | unset (cell skipped); K=4 when the value does not parse | gemma-gate batched-verify self-consistency, the base of `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE2`: a tokenwise reference, then one `decode_step_t` over the last K tokens; every position's argmax must match (`gemma_gate.rs`). |  |
| `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE2=K` (gemma-gate) | unset (cell skipped); K defaults to 2 when unparsable | `gemma-gate` chained batched-verify oracle: prefix tokenwise, then two back-to-back `decode_step_t` calls; per-position argmax must match the tokenwise chain. `MEMRA_VERIFY_GATE2_DEV=1` runs the device-token verify arm, the spec round's exact path. |  |

## Removed doors

Every name below was a door and is gone. The boot audit refuses each one and prints the heading it sits under, so a launcher that still sets one fails at boot instead of being ignored. The narrative of each removal is in [FLAGS-HISTORY.md](FLAGS-HISTORY.md), under the same heading.

### Removed doors, 2026-11-10 (`MEMRA_SERVE_BATCH`: the batched scheduler is the only served path; the server arm of the async chain went with the round-robin scheduler)

Retired: the batched scheduler is the only served path. The round-robin scheduler broke spec-on equals spec-off, and the async device chain it hosted was model-specific and never a default. Receipts: [serve-batch-removal-20261110](../research/serve-batch-removal-20261110/).

| Name | Record |
|---|---|
| `MEMRA_SERVE_BATCH` | [ledger](FLAGS-HISTORY.md#removed-doors-2026-11-10-memra_serve_batch-the-batched-scheduler-is-the-only-served-path-the-server-arm-of-the-async-chain-went-with-the-round-robin-scheduler) |

### Removed doors, 2026-11-09 (`MEMRA_SPEC_GDN_PACKED`: the packed GDN verify is the only path)

Rollback seam of the packed GDN verify, default since 2026-09-08 on bit-identical receipts and never used to roll back. The winner's seam was deleted; the packed path is the only one. Receipts: [gdn-packed-seam](../research/gb10-decode-20261109/gdn-packed-seam/), [run-spec-unpacked.txt](../research/gb10-decode-20261109/cols-extra-5090/run-spec-unpacked.txt).
